!important;border-bottom:1px solid #ab47bc}.vocab-subcontent-4{padding:12px;background:#fff;line-height:1.6;font-size:13px;color:#444}</style><div class="vocab-accordion"><details class="vocab-item"><summary>Тема 4: Food and Drinks (Еда и напитки)</summary><div class="vocab-content"><details class="vocab-subitem-1"><summary>Подтема 1: Basic food products (vegetables, fruits, meat, dairy, bread)</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-1"><strong>1. fresh vegetables / fruits</strong> — свежие овощи / фрукты<br><em>Пример: I eat fresh vegetables and fruits every day to stay healthy.</em><br><br><strong>2. organic food</strong> — органические продукты (без химикатов)<br><em>Пример: My mum prefers to buy organic food from the local market.</em><br><br><strong>3. dairy products</strong> — молочные продукты<br><em>Пример: Dairy products like milk and cheese are rich in calcium.</em><br><br><strong>4. whole grain bread</strong> — цельнозерновой хлеб<br><em>Пример: Whole grain bread is healthier than white bread.</em><br><br><strong>5. lean meat</strong> — нежирное мясо<br><em>Пример: Chicken and turkey are examples of lean meat.</em><br><br><strong>6. to be in season</strong> — быть в сезоне (о фруктах/овощах)<br><em>Пример: Strawberries are in season in summer, so they are cheap and tasty.</em><br><br><strong>7. to be out of season</strong> — быть не в сезоне<br><em>Пример: Oranges are out of season now, so they are expensive.</em><br><br><strong>8. to be ripe / unripe</strong> — быть спелым / незрелым<br><em>Пример: This banana is not ripe yet, it's still green.</em><br><br><strong>9. to be stale / fresh</strong> — быть чёрствым / свежим<br><em>Пример: Don't eat that bread, it's stale! Buy fresh bread instead.</em><br><br><strong>10. to be frozen / canned</strong> — быть замороженным / консервированным<br><em>Пример: We have frozen vegetables and canned tomatoes in the cupboard.</em><br><br><strong>11. to be rich in vitamins</strong> — быть богатым витаминами<br><em>Пример: Carrots are rich in vitamins and good for your eyes.</em><br><br><strong>12. to contain (sugar / fat)</strong> — содержать (сахар / жир)<br><em>Пример: This chocolate bar contains a lot of sugar and fat.</em><br><br><strong>13. to be low in calories</strong> — быть низкокалорийным<br><em>Пример: Salads are low in calories and help you lose weight.</em><br><br><strong>14. to be high in protein</strong> — быть с высоким содержанием белка<br><em>Пример: Eggs and fish are high in protein, which is good for muscles.</em><br><br><strong>15. to be gluten-free / lactose-free</strong> — быть без глютена / без лактозы<br><em>Пример: My friend is allergic to gluten, so she eats gluten-free bread.</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-2"><summary>Подтема 2: Beverages, sweets, and desserts</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-2"><strong>1. to quench thirst</strong> — утолять жажду<br><em>Пример: Water is the best drink to quench your thirst on a hot day.</em><br><br><strong>2. to be fizzy / carbonated</strong> — быть газированным<br><em>Пример: I don't drink fizzy drinks like cola because they are unhealthy.</em><br><br><strong>3. to be sugar-free</strong> — быть без сахара<br><em>Пример: My grandma drinks sugar-free tea because she has diabetes.</em><br><br><strong>4. to have a sweet tooth</strong> — любить сладкое<br><em>Пример: I have a sweet tooth, so I can't resist chocolate and cakes.</em><br><br><strong>5. to crave (something sweet)</strong> — сильно хотеть (чего-то сладкого)<br><em>Пример: I'm craving ice cream right now, let's go to the café!</em><br><br><strong>6. to indulge in (sweets)</strong> — позволить себе (сладости), побаловать себя<br><em>Пример: On my birthday, I indulge in my favourite chocolate cake.</em><br><br><strong>7. to be addicted to (sugar / coffee)</strong> — быть зависимым от (сахара / кофе)<br><em>Пример: My dad is addicted to coffee, he drinks five cups a day.</em><br><br><strong>8. a piece of cake / pie</strong> — кусок торта / пирога<br><em>Пример: Can I have a piece of apple pie for dessert, please?</em><br><br><strong>9. a bar of chocolate</strong> — плитка шоколада<br><em>Пример: I bought a bar of dark chocolate from the shop.</em><br><br><strong>10. a scoop of ice cream</strong> — шарик мороженого<br><em>Пример: I'd like two scoops of vanilla ice cream in a cone, please.</em><br><br><strong>11. to sip (tea / coffee)</strong> — пить маленькими глотками<br><em>Пример: My mum likes to sip hot tea while reading a book.</em><br><br><strong>12. to gulp down (a drink)</strong> — выпить залпом<br><em>Пример: He was so thirsty that he gulped down the whole bottle of water.</em><br><br><strong>13. to be refreshing</strong> — быть освежающим<br><em>Пример: Lemonade is very refreshing on a hot summer day.</em><br><br><strong>14. to be warming</strong> — быть согревающим<br><em>Пример: Hot chocolate is warming and perfect for cold winter evenings.</em><br><br><strong>15. to be homemade</strong> — быть домашним (приготовленным дома)<br><em>Пример: My grandmother's homemade cookies are the best in the world!</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-3"><summary>Подтема 3: Meals (breakfast, lunch, dinner) and table setting</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-3"><strong>1. to have / eat breakfast / lunch / dinner</strong> — завтракать / обедать / ужинать<br><em>Пример: We usually have dinner at 7 p.m. when my dad comes home from work.</em><br><br><strong>2. to skip a meal</strong> — пропустить приём пищи<br><em>Пример: I never skip breakfast because I need energy for school.</em><br><br><strong>3. to grab a bite (to eat)</strong> — перекусить на ходу<br><em>Пример: I'm in a hurry, so I'll just grab a bite at the café.</em><br><br><strong>4. to have a snack</strong> — перекусить<br><em>Пример: I usually have a snack between lunch and dinner, like an apple or nuts.</em><br><br><strong>5. to set / lay the table</strong> — накрывать на стол<br><em>Пример: Can you set the table for four people, please?</em><br><br><strong>6. to clear the table</strong> — убирать со стола<br><em>Пример: After dinner, we all help to clear the table and wash the dishes.</em><br><br><strong>7. to serve (food)</strong> — подавать (еду)<br><em>Пример: The waiter served us delicious pasta with tomato sauce.</em><br><br><strong>8. to be hungry / thirsty / full</strong> — быть голодным / испытывать жажду / быть сытым<br><em>Пример: I'm full, thank you, I can't eat another bite!</em><br><br><strong>9. to be starving</strong> — быть очень голодным<br><em>Пример: I'm starving! Let's order pizza right now.</em><br><br><strong>10. to have an appetite</strong> — иметь аппетит<br><em>Пример: I have a good appetite today, I could eat a horse!</em><br><br><strong>11. to lose one's appetite</strong> — потерять аппетит<br><em>Пример: I lost my appetite when I saw the dirty kitchen.</em><br><br><strong>12. to be fussy / picky about food</strong> — быть привередливым в еде<br><em>Пример: My little brother is very fussy about food, he only eats pasta and chips.</em><br><br><strong>13. to try / taste (something)</strong> — попробовать (что-то)<br><em>Пример: You should try this soup, it's delicious!</em><br><br><strong>14. to be tasty / delicious / yummy</strong> — быть вкусным<br><em>Пример: This cake is absolutely delicious, can I have the recipe?</em><br><br><strong>15. to be disgusting / awful</strong> — быть отвратительным<br><em>Пример: The food in that restaurant was disgusting, I won't go there again.</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-4"><summary>Подтема 4: Grocery shopping, purchases, and café/restaurant menus</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-4"><strong>1. to go grocery shopping</strong> — ходить за продуктами<br><em>Пример: We go grocery shopping every Saturday morning at the supermarket.</em><br><br><strong>2. to make a shopping list</strong> — составить список покупок<br><em>Пример: Always make a shopping list so you don't forget anything important.</em><br><br><strong>3. to buy in bulk</strong> — покупать оптом, в больших количествах<br><em>Пример: It's cheaper to buy rice and pasta in bulk.</em><br><br><strong>4. to be on sale / on offer</strong> — быть на распродаже / по акции<br><em>Пример: These apples are on sale today, buy two kilos for the price of one!</em><br><br><strong>5. to be out of stock</strong> — отсутствовать в продаже<br><em>Пример: I'm sorry, but we're out of stock of that brand of milk.</em><br><br><strong>6. to pay in cash / by card</strong> — платить наличными / картой<br><em>Пример: Can I pay by card, please? I don't have any cash.</em><br><br><strong>7. to get a receipt</strong> — получить чек<br><em>Пример: Always get a receipt in case you need to return something.</em><br><br><strong>8. to queue / wait in line</strong> — стоять в очереди<br><em>Пример: There was a long queue at the checkout, so I waited for ten minutes.</em><br><br><strong>9. to read the label</strong> — читать этикетку<br><em>Пример: I always read the label to check the expiry date and ingredients.</em><br><br><strong>10. to be past its sell-by date</strong> — быть просроченным<br><em>Пример: Don't eat that yoghurt, it's past its sell-by date!</em><br><br><strong>11. to order from the menu</strong> — заказать по меню<br><em>Пример: We ordered from the menu and chose pasta and salad.</em><br><br><strong>12. to ask for the bill / check</strong> — попросить счёт<br><em>Пример: Could we have the bill, please? We'd like to pay.</em><br><br><strong>13. to leave a tip</strong> — оставлять чаевые<br><em>Пример: In some countries, it's customary to leave a tip for good service.</em><br><br><strong>14. to be vegetarian / vegan</strong> — быть вегетарианцем / веганом<br><em>Пример: My friend is vegetarian, so she doesn't eat meat or fish.</em><br><br><strong>15. to have an allergy to (nuts / dairy)</strong> — иметь аллергию на (орехи / молочные продукты)<br><em>Пример: I have an allergy to nuts, so I can't eat this cake.</em></div></details></div></details><details class="vocab-item"><summary>Тема 5: Clothes and Appearance (Одежда и внешний вид)</summary><div class="vocab-content"><details class="vocab-subitem-1"><summary>Подтема 1: Everyday, sports, and festive clothing</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-1"><strong>1. casual clothes</strong> — повседневная одежда<br><em>Пример: I usually wear casual clothes like jeans and a T-shirt at the weekend.</em><br><br><strong>2. to dress up</strong> — наряжаться, надевать красивую одежду<br><em>Пример: We like to dress up for school parties and take lots of photos.</em><br><br><strong>3. to dress down</strong> — одеваться просто, в повседневном стиле<br><em>Пример: On Fridays, our school allows students to dress down and wear jeans.</em><br><br><strong>4. sportswear / a tracksuit</strong> — спортивная одежда / спортивный костюм<br><em>Пример: You must wear sportswear for your PE lessons at school.</em><br><br><strong>5. a festive outfit / party dress</strong> — праздничный наряд / платье для вечеринки<br><em>Пример: She bought a beautiful festive outfit for the New Year concert.</em><br><br><strong>6. to put on</strong> — надевать (на себя)<br><em>Пример: It is cold outside, so put on your warm jacket and hat.</em><br><br><strong>7. to take off</strong> — снимать (одежду)<br><em>Пример: Please take off your dirty shoes when you enter the house.</em><br><br><strong>8. to wear</strong> — носить (быть одетым во что-то)<br><em>Пример: My brother wears a school uniform from Monday to Friday.</em><br><br><strong>9. to change (one's clothes)</strong> — переодеться<br><em>Пример: I need to change my clothes after playing football in the rain.</em><br><br><strong>10. to be in fashion / trendy</strong> — быть в моде / модным<br><em>Пример: Baggy jeans are very trendy among teenagers right now.</em><br><br><strong>11. to be out of fashion</strong> — выйти из моды<br><em>Пример: I don't wear those old shoes anymore, they are out of fashion.</em><br><br><strong>12. to suit someone</strong> — идти к лицу, подходить (о цвете или фасоне)<br><em>Пример: The colour blue really suits you, it matches your eyes!</em><br><br><strong>13. to match</strong> — сочетаться (по цвету или стилю)<br><em>Пример: Your red scarf perfectly matches your red gloves.</em><br><br><strong>14. to keep warm</strong> — согреваться<br><em>Пример: Wear a thick jumper to keep warm in winter.</em><br><br><strong>15. to wrap up warm</strong> — тепло одеваться, кутаться<br><em>Пример: Wrap up warm before you go out, it is snowing heavily!</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-2"><summary>Подтема 2: Footwear and accessories (bags, umbrellas, belts, jewelry)</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-2"><strong>1. a pair of shoes / boots</strong> — пара туфель / ботинок<br><em>Пример: I need to buy a new pair of sports shoes for the gym.</em><br><br><strong>2. comfortable trainers / sneakers</strong> — удобные кроссовки<br><em>Пример: I wear comfortable trainers because I walk a lot every day.</em><br><br><strong>3. to tie shoelaces</strong> — завязывать шнурки<br><em>Пример: My little sister is learning how to tie her shoelaces.</em><br><br><strong>4. to wear high heels</strong> — носить обувь на высоком каблуке<br><em>Пример: My mother wears high heels when she goes to work.</em><br><br><strong>5. flat shoes / ballet pumps</strong> — обувь на плоской подошве / балетки<br><em>Пример: Flat shoes are much better for your feet than high heels.</em><br><br><strong>6. to take off one's shoes</strong> — разуваться<br><em>Пример: In many Asian countries, people take off their shoes before entering a home.</em><br><br><strong>7. a rucksack / backpack</strong> — рюкзак<br><em>Пример: I carry all my heavy school books in my black rucksack.</em><br><br><strong>8. a handbag / a purse</strong> — женская сумочка / кошелёк<br><em>Пример: My mum keeps her keys and phone in her small handbag.</em><br><br><strong>9. to wear jewellery</strong> — носить украшения<br><em>Пример: Our school rules say we cannot wear jewellery during lessons.</em><br><br><strong>10. a silver / gold ring</strong> — серебряное / золотое кольцо<br><em>Пример: My grandmother gave me a beautiful silver ring for my birthday.</em><br><br><strong>11. a necklace / a bracelet</strong> — ожерелье / браслет<br><em>Пример: She made a colourful bracelet out of beads for her friend.</em><br><br><strong>12. to wear a scarf and gloves</strong> — носить шарф и перчатки<br><em>Пример: Don't forget to wear a scarf and gloves when you build a snowman.</em><br><br><strong>13. a woolly hat / beanie</strong> — шерстяная шапка / вязаная шапочка<br><em>Пример: I always wear a woolly hat in winter so my ears don't freeze.</em><br><br><strong>14. an umbrella / a raincoat</strong> — зонт / дождевик<br><em>Пример: Take an umbrella or a raincoat, the forecast says it will rain.</em><br><br><strong>15. a belt / a tie</strong> — ремень / галстук<br><em>Пример: My dad always wears a suit and a tie for important meetings.</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-3"><summary>Подтема 3: Detailed physical description of a person (height, eyes, hair)</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-3"><strong>1. to be of medium height</strong> — быть среднего роста<br><em>Пример: My best friend is of medium height, she is neither tall nor short.</em><br><br><strong>2. to be tall / short</strong> — быть высоким / низким<br><em>Пример: My basketball coach is very tall, he is almost two metres!</em><br><br><strong>3. to be slim / thin / plump</strong> — быть стройным / худым / полным<br><em>Пример: She does a lot of sports, so she is very slim and fit.</em><br><br><strong>4. to be well-built / muscular</strong> — быть хорошо сложенным / мускулистым<br><em>Пример: My older brother goes to the gym and is quite muscular now.</em><br><br><strong>5. to have short / long / shoulder-length hair</strong> — иметь короткие / длинные / волосы до плеч<br><em>Пример: My sister has beautiful shoulder-length hair.</em><br><br><strong>6. straight / wavy / curly hair</strong> — прямые / волнистые / кудрявые волосы<br><em>Пример: It takes me a long time to brush my curly hair in the morning.</em><br><br><strong>7. to have a fringe (UK) / bangs (US)</strong> — иметь чёлку<br><em>Пример: I want to cut a fringe because my hair gets into my eyes.</em><br><br><strong>8. to dye one's hair</strong> — красить волосы<br><em>Пример: My aunt likes to dye her hair bright red in the summer.</em><br><br><strong>9. to have a ponytail / pigtails</strong> — носить хвост / косички<br><em>Пример: She tied her hair into a ponytail before going for a run.</em><br><br><strong>10. to have freckles</strong> — иметь веснушки<br><em>Пример: The little boy has cute freckles all over his nose and cheeks.</em><br><br><strong>11. to have a beard / moustache</strong> — носить бороду / усы<br><em>Пример: My history teacher has a thick grey beard.</em><br><br><strong>12. to look young / old for one's age</strong> — выглядеть молодо / старо для своего возраста<br><em>Пример: My 40-year-old mum looks very young for her age.</em><br><br><strong>13. to wear glasses / braces</strong> — носить очки / брекеты (на зубах)<br><em>Пример: I wear glasses for reading, and my friend wears braces.</em><br><br><strong>14. to have a tattoo / piercing</strong> — иметь татуировку / пирсинг<br><em>Пример: Teenagers often want to get a piercing, but parents usually say no.</em><br><br><strong>15. to have a nice smile / bright eyes</strong> — иметь красивую улыбку / яркие глаза<br><em>Пример: She is very pretty and has a really nice, friendly smile.</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-4"><summary>Подтема 4: Clothes shopping (sizes, fitting room, prices, materials)</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-4"><strong>1. to go clothes shopping</strong> — ходить за покупками одежды<br><em>Пример: We need to go clothes shopping because I have grown out of my winter coat.</em><br><br><strong>2. to try on (a dress / shirt)</strong> — примерять (платье / рубашку)<br><em>Пример: You should try on these jeans before you buy them.</em><br><br><strong>3. a fitting room / changing room</strong> — примерочная<br><em>Пример: The fitting rooms are at the back of the shop, near the mirrors.</em><br><br><strong>4. to fit someone</strong> — быть в пору, подходить по размеру<br><em>Пример: These shoes are too small, they don't fit me at all.</em><br><br><strong>5. to be too tight / loose</strong> — быть слишком тесным / свободным<br><em>Пример: This T-shirt is too loose, do you have it in a smaller size?</em><br><br><strong>6. What size are you?</strong> — Какой у вас размер?<br><em>Пример: The shop assistant asked me, "What size are you?"</em><br><br><strong>7. to be a size M (medium)</strong> — носить размер М (средний)<br><em>Пример: I am usually a size S, but this brand is small, so I need an M.</em><br><br><strong>8. to be on sale / half-price</strong> — быть на распродаже / за полцены<br><em>Пример: I love buying winter clothes in spring when they are on sale.</em><br><br><strong>9. to cost a fortune</strong> — стоить целое состояние (быть очень дорогим)<br><em>Пример: That designer handbag costs a fortune, I can't afford it.</em><br><br><strong>10. to be good value for money</strong> — быть выгодной покупкой (соотношение цена/качество)<br><em>Пример: These boots are expensive, but they are good value for money because they last for years.</em><br><br><strong>11. to pay in cash / by card</strong> — платить наличными / картой<br><em>Пример: I don't have enough cash, can I pay by card?</em><br><br><strong>12. to ask for a refund</strong> — просить возврат денег<br><em>Пример: The jumper had a hole in it, so I took it back and asked for a refund.</em><br><br><strong>13. to keep the receipt</strong> — сохранить чек<br><em>Пример: Always keep the receipt in case you need to return the item.</em><br><br><strong>14. to return / exchange an item</strong> — вернуть / обменять вещь<br><em>Пример: The shirt was the wrong colour, so I went back to exchange it.</em><br><br><strong>15. a bargain / a good deal</strong> — выгодная покупка / удачная сделка<br><em>Пример: I got this winter coat for only 20 pounds, it was a real bargain!</em></div></details></div></details><details class="vocab-item"><summary>Тема 6: School and Education (Школа и образование)</summary><div class="vocab-content"><details class="vocab-subitem-1"><summary>Подтема 1: School subjects and class timetables</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-1"><strong>1. school timetable / schedule</strong> — школьное расписание<br><em>Пример: I check my school timetable every evening to pack my bag for the next day.</em><br><br><strong>2. core subjects</strong> — основные предметы (обязательные)<br><em>Пример: Maths, Russian, and English are core subjects in our school.</em><br><br><strong>3. optional / elective subjects</strong> — предметы по выбору<br><em>Пример: In the 9th grade, students can choose optional subjects like art or computer science.</em><br><br><strong>4. to have a double lesson</strong> — иметь сдвоенный урок<br><em>Пример: We have a double lesson of chemistry on Tuesdays, so we spend two hours in the lab.</em><br><br><strong>5. a break / recess</strong> — перемена<br><em>Пример: I like to chat with my friends in the corridor during the break.</em><br><br><strong>6. a lunch break</strong> — обеденный перерыв / большая перемена<br><em>Пример: We have a long lunch break at 12 o'clock to eat in the school canteen.</em><br><br><strong>7. to be good / bad at (a subject)</strong> — преуспевать / не успевать по предмету<br><em>Пример: I am very good at history, but I am bad at physics.</em><br><br><strong>8. to fail an exam / a test</strong> — провалить экзамен / тест (получить неуд)<br><em>Пример: He didn't study for the maths test and failed it.</em><br><br><strong>9. to pass an exam</strong> — сдать экзамен (успешно)<br><em>Пример: She studied hard and passed her English exam with an excellent mark.</em><br><br><strong>10. to get a good / bad mark (grade)</strong> — получить хорошую / плохую оценку<br><em>Пример: I was happy to get a good mark for my project.</em><br><br><strong>11. to skip a class / play truant</strong> — прогуливать урок<br><em>Пример: If you skip a class, your parents will be called to the headteacher's office.</em><br><br><strong>12. to be top of the class</strong> — быть лучшим учеником в классе<br><em>Пример: Maria is always top of the class because she reads a lot.</em><br><br><strong>13. to fall behind</strong> — отставать (в учебе)<br><em>Пример: I was ill for two weeks and fell behind with my schoolwork.</em><br><br><strong>14. to catch up</strong> — навёрстывать упущенное<br><em>Пример: My teacher helps me to catch up after my illness.</em><br><br><strong>15. a form tutor / homeroom teacher</strong> — классный руководитель<br><em>Пример: Our form tutor organizes interesting trips for us every term.</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-2"><summary>Подтема 2: School supplies and classroom equipment</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-2"><strong>1. a pencil case</strong> — пенал<br><em>Пример: I keep all my pens, pencils, and rubbers in my new pencil case.</em><br><br><strong>2. a ruler / a set square</strong> — линейка / угольник<br><em>Пример: You need a ruler and a set square to draw straight lines and right angles in geometry.</em><br><br><strong>3. a rubber (UK) / eraser (US)</strong> — ластик<br><em>Пример: Can I borrow your rubber? I made a mistake in my drawing.</em><br><br><strong>4. a sharpener</strong> — точилка<br><em>Пример: My pencil is blunt, I need to use the sharpener.</em><br><br><strong>5. a highlighter</strong> — маркер-текстовыделитель<br><em>Пример: I use a yellow highlighter to mark important rules in my textbook.</em><br><br><strong>6. a folder / a ring binder</strong> — папка / скоросшиватель<br><em>Пример: Keep all your worksheets in a ring binder so you don't lose them.</em><br><br><strong>7. a whiteboard / an interactive board</strong> — маркерная / интерактивная доска<br><em>Пример: The teacher uses the interactive board to show us educational videos.</em><br><br><strong>8. a piece of chalk</strong> — кусок мела<br><em>Пример: The teacher wrote the date on the blackboard with a piece of white chalk.</em><br><br><strong>9. to hand in / submit (homework)</strong> — сдавать (домашнюю работу)<br><em>Пример: Please hand in your essays at the end of the lesson.</em><br><br><strong>10. to hand out (worksheets)</strong> — раздавать (раздаточный материал)<br><em>Пример: The teacher asked me to hand out the test papers to the class.</em><br><br><strong>11. to take notes</strong> — делать заметки, конспектировать<br><em>Пример: It is important to take notes during the lecture so you can revise later.</em><br><br><strong>12. to look up (a word in a dictionary)</strong> — искать (слово в словаре)<br><em>Пример: If you don't know the translation, look it up in your dictionary.</em><br><br><strong>13. to cross out / rub out</strong> — зачёркивать / стирать<br><em>Пример: Don't cross out your mistakes with a pen, use a rubber instead.</em><br><br><strong>14. a desk / a locker</strong> — парта / шкафчик для вещей<br><em>Пример: I keep my heavy textbooks and sports kit in my school locker.</em><br><br><strong>15. a calculator</strong> — калькулятор<br><em>Пример: We are allowed to use a calculator during the difficult maths exam.</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-3"><summary>Подтема 3: School events, extracurricular clubs, and school rules</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-3"><strong>1. extracurricular activities</strong> — внеклассные занятия<br><em>Пример: Our school offers many extracurricular activities, like robotics and drama.</em><br><br><strong>2. to join a club / society</strong> — вступить в клуб / кружок<br><em>Пример: I decided to join the photography club because I love taking pictures.</em><br><br><strong>3. a school play / concert</strong> — школьная пьеса / концерт<br><em>Пример: My sister got the main role in the annual school play.</em><br><br><strong>4. a sports day</strong> — день спорта<br><em>Пример: We have a sports day in May where we compete in running and jumping.</em><br><br><strong>5. a school trip / excursion</strong> — школьная поездка / экскурсия<br><em>Пример: Next week, our class is going on a school trip to the history museum.</em><br><br><strong>6. a school uniform</strong> — школьная форма<br><em>Пример: In our school, we must wear a strict uniform: a white shirt and dark trousers.</em><br><br><strong>7. to wear a uniform</strong> — носить форму<br><em>Пример: I don't mind wearing a uniform because it saves time in the morning.</em><br><br><strong>8. school rules</strong> — школьные правила<br><em>Пример: All students must read the school rules at the beginning of the year.</em><br><br><strong>9. to break the rules</strong> — нарушать правила<br><em>Пример: If you break the rules, you will have to speak to the headteacher.</em><br><br><strong>10. to get detention / be punished</strong> — остаться после уроков (в качестве наказания) / быть наказанным<br><em>Пример: He got detention for a week because he was rude to the teacher.</em><br><br><strong>11. to be rewarded / get a prize</strong> — быть вознаграждённым / получить приз<br><em>Пример: Students who read the most books are rewarded with a certificate at the end of the year.</em><br><br><strong>12. a school council</strong> — школьный совет (ученическое самоуправление)<br><em>Пример: I was elected to the school council to represent my classmates.</em><br><br><strong>13. to take part in / participate in</strong> — принимать участие в<br><em>Пример: Everyone is encouraged to take part in the school charity marathon.</em><br><br><strong>14. to organize an event</strong> — организовывать мероприятие<br><em>Пример: The senior students organize a disco for the younger kids every winter.</em><br><br><strong>15. a school charity fair</strong> — школьная благотворительная ярмарка<br><em>Пример: We baked cakes to sell at the school charity fair and raised money for the animal shelter.</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-4"><summary>Подтема 4: Learning foreign languages and doing homework</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-4"><strong>1. to learn a foreign language</strong> — изучать иностранный язык<br><em>Пример: Learning a foreign language opens up many opportunities for travelling and work.</em><br><br><strong>2. mother tongue / native language</strong> — родной язык<br><em>Пример: Russian is my mother tongue, but I also study English and Spanish.</em><br><br><strong>3. to be fluent in</strong> — свободно владеть (языком)<br><em>Пример: My uncle lived in London for five years, so he is fluent in English.</em><br><br><strong>4. to have a good accent</strong> — иметь хорошее произношение<br><em>Пример: She watches a lot of English movies and has a very good accent.</em><br><br><strong>5. to memorize vocabulary</strong> — запоминать новые слова<br><em>Пример: I use flashcards to memorize vocabulary for my English tests.</em><br><br><strong>6. to practise speaking / listening</strong> — практиковать говорение / аудирование<br><em>Пример: We practise speaking in pairs during our English lessons.</em><br><br><strong>7. to do homework</strong> — делать домашнее задание<br><em>Пример: I usually do my homework right after school while my brain is still active.</em><br><br><strong>8. to hand in homework on time</strong> — сдавать домашнюю работу вовремя<br><em>Пример: The teacher gave me a bad mark because I didn't hand in my homework on time.</em><br><br><strong>9. to copy from a classmate</strong> — списывать у одноклассника<br><em>Пример: It is dishonest to copy from a classmate, you should do the work yourself.</em><br><br><strong>10. to revise for a test</strong> — повторять материал к тесту<br><em>Пример: I need to revise all the irregular verbs for tomorrow's test.</em><br><br><strong>11. to learn by heart</strong> — учить наизусть<br><em>Пример: We had to learn a beautiful poem by heart for the literature lesson.</em><br><br><strong>12. to write an essay / a report</strong> — писать эссе / доклад<br><em>Пример: For our history project, we have to write a short report about ancient Egypt.</em><br><br><strong>13. to give a presentation</strong> — делать презентацию / выступать с докладом<br><em>Пример: I was very nervous when I had to give a presentation in front of the whole class.</em><br><br><strong>14. to make a mistake</strong> — делать ошибку<br><em>Пример: Don't be afraid to make a mistake when you speak a new language.</em><br><br><strong>15. to correct a mistake</strong> — исправлять ошибку<br><em>Пример: The teacher used a red pen to correct the mistakes in my dictation.</em></div></details></div></details></div></div>

!important}.vocab-subitem-4>summary::-webkit-details-marker{display:none}.vocab-subitem-4>summary::after{content:"+";font-size:18px;color:#000;font-weight:bold}.vocab-subitem-4>summary::after{content:"−"}.vocab-subitem-4>summary{background:#ba68c8 !important;border-bottom:1px solid #ab47bc}.vocab-subcontent-4{padding:12px;background:#fff;line-height:1.6;font-size:13px;color:#444}</style><div class="vocab-accordion"><details class="vocab-item"><summary>Тема 7: Free Time and Hobbies (Досуг и хобби)</summary><div class="vocab-content"><details class="vocab-subitem-1"><summary>Подтема 1: Interests and hobbies (music, drawing, collecting, dancing)</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-1"><strong>1. to have a hobby</strong> — иметь хобби / увлечение<br><em>Пример: My brother has an unusual hobby: he builds model ships.</em><br><br><strong>2. to be keen on / to be fond of</strong> — увлекаться чем-либо, очень любить что-то<br><em>Пример: I am really keen on drawing, I do it every evening.</em><br><br><strong>3. to take up a new hobby</strong> — начать заниматься новым хобби<br><em>Пример: I decided to take up photography this summer.</em><br><br><strong>4. to give up a hobby</strong> — бросить хобби, перестать чем-то заниматься<br><em>Пример: She gave up playing the piano because she didn't have enough time.</em><br><br><strong>5. to collect stamps / coins</strong> — коллекционировать марки / монеты<br><em>Пример: My grandfather collects old coins from different countries.</em><br><br><strong>6. to play a musical instrument</strong> — играть на музыкальном инструменте<br><em>Пример: Do you play any musical instruments? I play the guitar.</em><br><br><strong>7. to do arts and crafts</strong> — заниматься рукоделием, декоративно-прикладным творчеством<br><em>Пример: We do arts and crafts at school, like making pottery and knitting.</em><br><br><strong>8. to paint / draw pictures</strong> — рисовать картины (красками / карандашами)<br><em>Пример: She loves to paint pictures of nature in the park.</em><br><br><strong>9. to take photographs</strong> — фотографировать, заниматься фотографией<br><em>Пример: I take photographs of my friends when we go for a walk.</em><br><br><strong>10. to sing in a choir</strong> — петь в хоре<br><em>Пример: My sister sings in a school choir and they perform at every concert.</em><br><br><strong>11. to act in a play</strong> — играть в спектакле / пьесе<br><em>Пример: He is very shy, but he likes to act in our school plays.</em><br><br><strong>12. to be interested in</strong> — интересоваться чем-либо<br><em>Пример: I am very interested in history and ancient civilizations.</em><br><br><strong>13. to share an interest</strong> — разделять интерес (иметь общее увлечение)<br><em>Пример: My best friend and I share an interest in robotics.</em><br><br><strong>14. to spend free time</strong> — проводить свободное время<br><em>Пример: How do you usually spend your free time at the weekend?</em><br><br><strong>15. to win a competition</strong> — победить в конкурсе / соревновании<br><em>Пример: She won a drawing competition and got a new set of paints.</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-2"><summary>Подтема 2: Reading books, going to the cinema, theatre, and concerts</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-2"><strong>1. to read a book / magazine</strong> — читать книгу / журнал<br><em>Пример: I prefer to read adventure books before going to sleep.</em><br><br><strong>2. to be a bookworm</strong> — быть «книжным червём» (заядлым читателем)<br><em>Пример: My classmate is a real bookworm, she reads a book every week.</em><br><br><strong>3. to borrow a book from the library</strong> — брать книгу в библиотеке<br><em>Пример: You can borrow up to five books from the school library at a time.</em><br><br><strong>4. to turn the page</strong> — перевернуть страницу<br><em>Пример: The story was so exciting that I couldn't stop turning the pages.</em><br><br><strong>5. to go to the cinema</strong> — ходить в кино<br><em>Пример: Let's go to the cinema on Saturday to watch the new cartoon.</em><br><br><strong>6. to watch a comedy / horror film</strong> — смотреть комедию / фильм ужасов<br><em>Пример: I don't like to watch horror films at night because I get scared.</em><br><br><strong>7. to buy tickets in advance</strong> — покупать билеты заранее<br><em>Пример: We bought tickets for the concert in advance to get the best seats.</em><br><br><strong>8. to be sold out</strong> — быть распроданным (о билетах)<br><em>Пример: We couldn't go to the theatre because the tickets were sold out.</em><br><br><strong>9. to watch a live performance</strong> — смотреть живое выступление<br><em>Пример: Watching a live performance is much better than watching it on TV.</em><br><br><strong>10. to clap one's hands / applaud</strong> — хлопать в ладоши / аплодировать<br><em>Пример: The audience stood up and applauded the actors at the end of the play.</em><br><br><strong>11. to go to a concert</strong> — ходить на концерт<br><em>Пример: My parents are going to a rock concert this Friday evening.</em><br><br><strong>12. to listen to live music</strong> — слушать живую музыку<br><em>Пример: In summer, we often go to the park to listen to live music.</em><br><br><strong>13. to sing along to a song</strong> — подпевать песне<br><em>Пример: Everyone in the car sang along to our favourite song.</em><br><br><strong>14. to be a fan of</strong> — быть фанатом / поклонником (кого-то или чего-то)<br><em>Пример: I am a big fan of this pop singer, I know all her songs.</em><br><br><strong>15. to get an autograph</strong> — взять автограф<br><em>Пример: I waited after the concert and managed to get an autograph from the band.</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-3"><summary>Подтема 3: The Internet, social media, and computer games</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-3"><strong>1. to surf the Internet</strong> — «сёрфить» в интернете, бродить по сайтам<br><em>Пример: I usually surf the Internet to find information for my school projects.</em><br><br><strong>2. to scroll through the feed</strong> — листать ленту (новостей или соцсетей)<br><em>Пример: She spends too much time scrolling through the feed on her phone.</em><br><br><strong>3. to post a photo / update</strong> — публиковать фото / обновление статуса<br><em>Пример: I posted a photo of my new puppy on my social media page.</em><br><br><strong>4. to chat with friends online</strong> — общаться с друзьями онлайн<br><em>Пример: We chat with friends online every evening using a messenger app.</em><br><br><strong>5. to send a voice message</strong> — отправлять голосовое сообщение<br><em>Пример: It is faster to send a voice message than to type a long text.</em><br><br><strong>6. to play online games</strong> — играть в онлайн-игры<br><em>Пример: My brother loves to play online games with his friends from other cities.</em><br><br><strong>7. to beat a level / boss</strong> — пройти уровень / победить босса (в игре)<br><em>Пример: It took me three hours to beat the final boss in this game!</em><br><br><strong>8. to download an app / game</strong> — скачивать приложение / игру<br><em>Пример: You need to download this app to learn new English words.</em><br><br><strong>9. to watch videos / vlogs</strong> — смотреть видео / влоги<br><em>Пример: I like watching educational vlogs about science and space.</em><br><br><strong>10. to subscribe to a channel</strong> — подписаться на канал<br><em>Пример: Don't forget to subscribe to my channel and click the bell icon!</em><br><br><strong>11. to leave a comment</strong> — оставлять комментарий<br><em>Пример: I left a nice comment under my friend's new video.</em><br><br><strong>12. to lose the Wi-Fi signal</strong> — потерять сигнал Wi-Fi<br><em>Пример: I was in the middle of a game when I suddenly lost the Wi-Fi signal.</em><br><br><strong>13. to run out of battery</strong> — разрядиться (о телефоне или ноутбуке)<br><em>Пример: My phone ran out of battery, so I couldn't call my mum.</em><br><br><strong>14. to be addicted to the screen</strong> — быть зависимым от экрана (гаджетов)<br><em>Пример: Doctors say that teenagers shouldn't be addicted to the screen.</em><br><br><strong>15. to take a digital detox</strong> — устроить цифровой детокс (отдых от гаджетов)<br><em>Пример: At the weekend, our family takes a digital detox and goes to the forest.</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-4"><summary>Подтема 4: Outdoor walks, picnics, and hanging out with friends</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-4"><strong>1. to go for a walk</strong> — идти на прогулку<br><em>Пример: After dinner, my family and I usually go for a walk in the park.</em><br><br><strong>2. to hang out with friends</strong> — тусоваться, неформально проводить время с друзьями<br><em>Пример: On Saturdays, I love to hang out with my friends at the shopping mall.</em><br><br><strong>3. to have a picnic</strong> — устраивать пикник<br><em>Пример: If the weather is warm, we will have a picnic by the river.</em><br><br><strong>4. to pack a picnic basket</strong> — собирать корзину для пикника<br><em>Пример: Mum packed a picnic basket with sandwiches, fruit, and juice.</em><br><br><strong>5. to ride a bike / cycle</strong> — кататься на велосипеде<br><em>Пример: We ride our bikes in the park every Sunday morning.</em><br><br><strong>6. to go rollerblading / skateboarding</strong> — кататься на роликах / скейтборде<br><em>Пример: There is a great place in the city centre to go skateboarding.</em><br><br><strong>7. to feed the birds / ducks</strong> — кормить птиц / уток<br><em>Пример: We took some bread to the pond to feed the ducks.</em><br><br><strong>8. to explore the neighbourhood</strong> — исследовать окрестности / свой район<br><em>Пример: When we moved to a new house, we spent the day exploring the neighbourhood.</em><br><br><strong>9. to catch up with friends</strong> — встретиться, чтобы поболтать и узнать новости<br><em>Пример: I haven't seen Anna for a month, we need to meet and catch up.</em><br><br><strong>10. to grab an ice cream / coffee</strong> — зайти за мороженым / кофе (перекусить на ходу)<br><em>Пример: Let's grab an ice cream on our way to the cinema.</em><br><br><strong>11. to go window shopping</strong> — ходить по магазинам, разглядывая витрины (без покупок)<br><em>Пример: We didn't have any money, so we just went window shopping.</em><br><br><strong>12. to meet up at the mall</strong> — встретиться в торговом центре<br><em>Пример: Let's meet up at the mall near the food court at 4 p.m.</em><br><br><strong>13. to play board games</strong> — играть в настольные игры<br><em>Пример: When it rains, we stay indoors and play board games like Monopoly.</em><br><br><strong>14. to tell jokes / funny stories</strong> — рассказывать шутки / забавные истории<br><em>Пример: My friend is great at telling jokes, he always makes us laugh.</em><br><br><strong>15. to have a great time</strong> — отлично проводить время<br><em>Пример: We had a great time at the beach yesterday!</em></div></details></div></details><details class="vocab-item"><summary>Тема 8: Sports and Healthy Lifestyle (Спорт и здоровый образ жизни)</summary><div class="vocab-content"><details class="vocab-subitem-1"><summary>Подтема 1: Popular sports and sports equipment</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-1"><strong>1. to play sports</strong> — заниматься (игровыми) видами спорта<br><em>Пример: I play sports like football and basketball with my friends after school.</em><br><br><strong>2. to do gymnastics / athletics</strong> — заниматься гимнастикой / лёгкой атлетикой<br><em>Пример: My sister does gymnastics twice a week, she is very flexible.</em><br><br><strong>3. to go swimming / cycling</strong> — ходить на плавание / кататься на велосипеде<br><em>Пример: We go swimming in the local pool every Saturday morning.</em><br><br><strong>4. a sports kit / uniform</strong> — спортивная форма / экипировка<br><em>Пример: Don't forget to pack your sports kit for the PE lesson tomorrow.</em><br><br><strong>5. sports equipment</strong> — спортивный инвентарь<br><em>Пример: The school bought new sports equipment for the gym, including balls and mats.</em><br><br><strong>6. a tennis racket</strong> — теннисная ракетка<br><em>Пример: I need to buy a new tennis racket because my old one is broken.</em><br><br><strong>7. to kick / throw / catch a ball</strong> — пинать / бросать / ловить мяч<br><em>Пример: The goalkeeper managed to catch the ball and save the goal.</em><br><br><strong>8. to score a goal</strong> — забить гол<br><em>Пример: Our team scored a goal in the last minute and won the match!</em><br><br><strong>9. to hit the target</strong> — попасть в цель<br><em>Пример: In archery, you need to aim carefully to hit the target.</em><br><br><strong>10. a swimming pool</strong> — плавательный бассейн<br><em>Пример: The indoor swimming pool is open all year round.</em><br><br><strong>11. a basketball court</strong> — баскетбольная площадка<br><em>Пример: There is a new basketball court in the park near my house.</em><br><br><strong>12. a football pitch</strong> — футбольное поле<br><em>Пример: The football pitch was wet after the rain, so the game was cancelled.</em><br><br><strong>13. to wear protective gear</strong> — носить защитную экипировку<br><em>Пример: You must wear protective gear, like a helmet and knee pads, when skateboarding.</em><br><br><strong>14. to be a team player</strong> — быть командным игроком<br><em>Пример: A good footballer must be a team player and pass the ball to others.</em><br><br><strong>15. to pass the ball</strong> — делать пас, передавать мяч<br><em>Пример: He didn't shoot; he decided to pass the ball to his teammate.</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-2"><summary>Подтема 2: Summer and winter sports, the Olympic Games</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-2"><strong>1. summer / winter sports</strong> — летние / зимние виды спорта<br><em>Пример: Swimming and tennis are popular summer sports, while skiing is a winter sport.</em><br><br><strong>2. to go skiing / snowboarding</strong> — кататься на лыжах / сноуборде<br><em>Пример: My family goes skiing in the mountains every February.</em><br><br><strong>3. to go ice skating</strong> — кататься на коньках<br><em>Пример: Let's go ice skating at the Red Square rink this weekend!</em><br><br><strong>4. an ice rink</strong> — ледовый каток<br><em>Пример: The indoor ice rink is a great place to practise figure skating.</em><br><br><strong>5. a ski resort</strong> — горнолыжный курорт<br><em>Пример: We spent the whole week at a beautiful ski resort in Sochi.</em><br><br><strong>6. to take part in the Olympics</strong> — участвовать в Олимпийских играх<br><em>Пример: Every athlete dreams to take part in the Olympics one day.</em><br><br><strong>7. an Olympic champion</strong> — олимпийский чемпион<br><em>Пример: The Olympic champion gave an interview after winning the race.</em><br><br><strong>8. to win a gold / silver / bronze medal</strong> — завоевать золотую / серебряную / бронзовую медаль<br><em>Пример: Our country won three gold medals in swimming this year.</em><br><br><strong>9. to break a world record</strong> — побить мировой рекорд<br><em>Пример: The runner trained hard and finally broke the world record.</em><br><br><strong>10. the Olympic torch / flame</strong> — олимпийский факел / огонь<br><em>Пример: The Olympic torch is carried by runners across the country before the Games.</em><br><br><strong>11. to host the Games</strong> — принимать Игры (быть страной-хозяйкой)<br><em>Пример: Paris will host the Summer Olympic Games in 2024.</em><br><br><strong>12. a Paralympic athlete</strong> — паралимпийский спортсмен<br><em>Пример: Paralympic athletes show incredible strength and determination.</em><br><br><strong>13. to compete against</strong> — соревноваться против<br><em>Пример: I like to compete against my brother in table tennis.</em><br><br><strong>14. a sports fan / supporter</strong> — спортивный болельщик / фанат<br><em>Пример: My dad is a big sports fan, he never misses a hockey match on TV.</em><br><br><strong>15. to cheer for a team</strong> — болеть за команду<br><em>Пример: The whole stadium stood up to cheer for our national team.</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-3"><summary>Подтема 3: Sports competitions, stadiums, and gyms</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-3"><strong>1. a sports competition / tournament</strong> — спортивное соревнование / турнир<br><em>Пример: Our school won first place in the regional sports competition.</em><br><br><strong>2. to take part in a match</strong> — участвовать в матче<br><em>Пример: I am very nervous because I take part in an important tennis match today.</em><br><br><strong>3. a referee / an umpire</strong> — судья (в футболе / в теннисе)<br><em>Пример: The referee showed a red card to the player for a bad foul.</em><br><br><strong>4. to blow the whistle</strong> — дуть в свисток (сигнал судьи)<br><em>Пример: The game stopped when the referee blew the whistle.</em><br><br><strong>5. to win / lose a match</strong> — выиграть / проиграть матч<br><em>Пример: It's not about whether you win or lose a match, it's about how you play.</em><br><br><strong>6. a draw / a tie</strong> — ничья<br><em>Пример: The match ended in a draw, both teams scored two goals.</em><br><br><strong>7. the final score</strong> — окончательный счёт<br><em>Пример: The final score was 3-1 in favour of the home team.</em><br><br><strong>8. a stadium</strong> — стадион<br><em>Пример: Thousands of fans filled the stadium to watch the final game.</em><br><br><strong>9. to work out at the gym</strong> — тренироваться в спортзале<br><em>Пример: My older brother works out at the gym to build his muscles.</em><br><br><strong>10. to lift weights</strong> — поднимать тяжести<br><em>Пример: You shouldn't lift weights that are too heavy for you.</em><br><br><strong>11. to do push-ups / sit-ups</strong> — отжиматься / качать пресс<br><em>Пример: Our PE teacher makes us do twenty push-ups and sit-ups every lesson.</em><br><br><strong>12. a personal trainer / coach</strong> — личный тренер<br><em>Пример: A good coach always motivates you to do your best.</em><br><br><strong>13. to warm up before exercise</strong> — делать разминку перед тренировкой<br><em>Пример: It is very important to warm up before exercise to avoid injuries.</em><br><br><strong>14. to cool down</strong> — делать заминку, успокаивать пульс<br><em>Пример: After running, you should walk slowly to cool down.</em><br><br><strong>15. to be in good shape / out of shape</strong> — быть в хорошей / плохой физической форме<br><em>Пример: I get tired quickly because I am a bit out of shape.</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-4"><summary>Подтема 4: Healthy habits, daily routines, and active leisure</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-4"><strong>1. a healthy lifestyle</strong> — здоровый образ жизни<br><em>Пример: Eating well and doing sports are the main parts of a healthy lifestyle.</em><br><br><strong>2. to keep fit / stay in shape</strong> — поддерживать физическую форму<br><em>Пример: I go jogging every morning to keep fit.</em><br><br><strong>3. to eat a balanced diet</strong> — питаться сбалансированно<br><em>Пример: To be healthy, you need to eat a balanced diet with lots of vegetables.</em><br><br><strong>4. to cut down on sugar / junk food</strong> — сократить потребление сахара / вредной еды<br><em>Пример: My doctor told me to cut down on sugar and fizzy drinks.</em><br><br><strong>5. to drink plenty of water</strong> — пить много воды<br><em>Пример: You should drink plenty of water, especially when you do sports.</em><br><br><strong>6. to get enough sleep</strong> — высыпаться, спать достаточно<br><em>Пример: Teenagers need to get enough sleep, at least 8-9 hours a night.</em><br><br><strong>7. to take a break</strong> — делать перерыв<br><em>Пример: If you study for a long time, remember to take a break and stretch.</em><br><br><strong>8. to go for a jog / run</strong> — выходить на пробежку<br><em>Пример: My dad goes for a jog in the park before breakfast.</em><br><br><strong>9. to do morning exercises</strong> — делать утреннюю зарядку<br><em>Пример: Doing morning exercises helps me to wake up and feel energetic.</em><br><br><strong>10. to spend time outdoors</strong> — проводить время на свежем воздухе<br><em>Пример: Doctors recommend spending at least two hours outdoors every day.</em><br><br><strong>11. to go hiking</strong> — ходить в поход, гулять по пересечённой местности<br><em>Пример: We love to go hiking in the mountains during the summer holidays.</em><br><br><strong>12. to breathe fresh air</strong> — дышать свежим воздухом<br><em>Пример: It's so nice to breathe fresh air in the forest away from the city.</em><br><br><strong>13. to reduce stress</strong> — снижать стресс<br><em>Пример: Yoga and meditation are great ways to reduce stress after a hard day.</em><br><br><strong>14. to feel energetic</strong> — чувствовать себя энергичным<br><em>Пример: A good breakfast makes me feel energetic for the whole school day.</em><br><br><strong>15. to avoid bad habits</strong> — избегать вредных привычек<br><em>Пример: It is important to avoid bad habits like smoking or eating too much fast food.</em></div></details></div></details><details class="vocab-item"><summary>Тема 9: Health and Body (Здоровье и части тела)</summary><div class="vocab-content"><details class="vocab-subitem-1"><summary>Подтема 1: Parts of the body and the human face</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-1"><strong>1. to have a pain in (one's back/leg)</strong> — испытывать боль в (спине/ноге)<br><em>Пример: I can't play football today, I have a pain in my leg.</em><br><br><strong>2. to break one's arm / leg</strong> — сломать руку / ногу<br><em>Пример: He fell off his bike and broke his arm last summer.</em><br><br><strong>3. to twist one's ankle</strong> — подвернуть лодыжку<br><em>Пример: Be careful on the stairs, you don't want to twist your ankle.</em><br><br><strong>4. to shrug one's shoulders</strong> — пожимать плечами (в знак незнания)<br><em>Пример: When I asked him where my book was, he just shrugged his shoulders.</em><br><br><strong>5. to shake one's head</strong> — качать головой (в знак несогласия)<br><em>Пример: She shook her head when I offered her more cake.</em><br><br><strong>6. to nod one's head</strong> — кивать головой (в знак согласия)<br><em>Пример: The teacher nodded his head when I gave the correct answer.</em><br><br><strong>7. to cross one's arms / legs</strong> — скрещивать руки / ноги<br><em>Пример: It is rude to sit with your arms crossed when someone is talking to you.</em><br><br><strong>8. to point one's finger</strong> — указывать пальцем<br><em>Пример: My mum always tells me not to point my finger at people.</em><br><br><strong>9. to clap one's hands</strong> — хлопать в ладоши<br><em>Пример: The children clapped their hands happily when they saw the clown.</em><br><br><strong>10. to have broad shoulders</strong> — иметь широкие плечи<br><em>Пример: My older brother swims a lot, so he has very broad shoulders.</em><br><br><strong>11. to clear one's throat</strong> — откашливаться (чтобы привлечь внимание или перед речью)<br><em>Пример: The headteacher cleared his throat before starting the speech.</em><br><br><strong>12. to get an insect bite</strong> — получить укус насекомого<br><em>Пример: I got an insect bite on my arm while we were in the forest.</em><br><br><strong>13. to scratch a mosquito bite</strong> — чесать укус комара<br><em>Пример: Try not to scratch the mosquito bite, or it will get worse.</em><br><br><strong>14. to have a good posture</strong> — иметь хорошую осанку<br><em>Пример: Sitting up straight at your desk helps you to have a good posture.</em><br><br><strong>15. to turn one's back</strong> — повернуться спиной<br><em>Пример: It's impolite to turn your back on someone when they are speaking.</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-2"><summary>Подтема 2: Well-being and common illnesses</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-2"><strong>1. to catch a cold</strong> — простудиться<br><em>Пример: Put on your jacket, or you will catch a cold in this wind!</em><br><br><strong>2. to have a high temperature / fever</strong> — иметь высокую температуру / жар<br><em>Пример: I can't go to school today because I have a high temperature.</em><br><br><strong>3. to have a sore throat</strong> — иметь больное горло<br><em>Пример: It hurts when I swallow, I think I have a sore throat.</em><br><br><strong>4. to have a runny / blocked nose</strong> — иметь насморк / заложенный нос<br><em>Пример: I have a blocked nose and can't smell anything.</em><br><br><strong>5. to cough and sneeze</strong> — кашлять и чихать<br><em>Пример: Please cover your mouth when you cough and sneeze.</em><br><br><strong>6. to have a headache / stomach ache</strong> — иметь головную боль / боль в животе<br><em>Пример: I ate too much candy and now I have a terrible stomach ache.</em><br><br><strong>7. to feel sick / dizzy</strong> — чувствовать тошноту / головокружение<br><em>Пример: I always feel sick when I read in a moving car.</em><br><br><strong>8. to feel under the weather</strong> — чувствовать себя неважно (плохо, нездоровится)<br><em>Пример: I'm feeling a bit under the weather today, I think I'll stay in bed.</em><br><br><strong>9. to be in pain</strong> — испытывать боль<br><em>Пример: The poor dog was in pain after the accident.</em><br><br><strong>10. to complain of (a pain)</strong> — жаловаться на (боль)<br><em>Пример: My grandfather often complains of a pain in his back.</em><br><br><strong>11. to get better / to recover</strong> — поправляться / выздоравливать<br><em>Пример: Take this medicine and you will get better in a few days.</em><br><br><strong>12. to get worse</strong> — становиться хуже (о состоянии здоровья)<br><em>Пример: If your cough gets worse, you must see a doctor.</em><br><br><strong>13. to have an allergic reaction</strong> — иметь аллергическую реакцию<br><em>Пример: She had an allergic reaction to the strawberries and got a rash.</em><br><br><strong>14. to get sunburnt</strong> — обгореть на солнце<br><em>Пример: Don't forget your hat, or you will get sunburnt at the beach.</em><br><br><strong>15. to feel exhausted</strong> — чувствовать себя истощённым / очень уставшим<br><em>Пример: After the long hike, we all felt completely exhausted.</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-3"><summary>Подтема 3: Doctor's visits, the pharmacy, and basic medicines</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-3"><strong>1. to make an appointment (with a doctor)</strong> — записаться на приём (к врачу)<br><em>Пример: My mum made an appointment with the dentist for tomorrow afternoon.</em><br><br><strong>2. to see a doctor</strong> — пойти к врачу / быть осмотренным врачом<br><em>Пример: You look very pale, you should go and see a doctor.</em><br><br><strong>3. to take one's temperature</strong> — измерять температуру<br><em>Пример: The nurse took my temperature and said it was 38 degrees.</em><br><br><strong>4. to check one's pulse / blood pressure</strong> — проверять пульс / артериальное давление<br><em>Пример: The doctor checked my pulse to see how fast my heart was beating.</em><br><br><strong>5. to prescribe medicine / pills</strong> — выписывать лекарство / таблетки<br><em>Пример: The doctor prescribed some medicine for my cough.</em><br><br><strong>6. to take medicine / pills</strong> — принимать лекарство / таблетки<br><em>Пример: You need to take these pills three times a day after meals.</em><br><br><strong>7. to get a prescription</strong> — получить рецепт<br><em>Пример: You can only buy these strong antibiotics if you get a prescription.</em><br><br><strong>8. to go to the pharmacy / chemist's</strong> — идти в аптеку<br><em>Пример: I need to go to the pharmacy to buy some vitamins.</em><br><br><strong>9. a painkiller</strong> — обезболивающее<br><em>Пример: I had a terrible toothache, so I took a painkiller.</em><br><br><strong>10. a cough syrup / mixture</strong> — сироп от кашля<br><em>Пример: This cough syrup tastes like strawberries, but it really helps.</em><br><br><strong>11. to put on a plaster / bandage</strong> — наклеить пластырь / наложить повязку<br><em>Пример: I cut my finger, can you help me put on a plaster?</em><br><br><strong>12. to give an injection</strong> — делать укол<br><em>Пример: I hate going to the hospital because they always give me an injection.</em><br><br><strong>13. to have a medical check-up</strong> — проходить медицинский осмотр<br><em>Пример: All students have to have a medical check-up at the beginning of the school year.</em><br><br><strong>14. to be off school (due to illness)</strong> — не ходить в школу (по болезни, быть на больничном)<br><em>Пример: Mark has been off school for a week because he had the flu.</em><br><br><strong>15. to follow the doctor's advice</strong> — следовать совету врача<br><em>Пример: If you want to recover quickly, you must follow the doctor's advice.</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-4"><summary>Подтема 4: Personal hygiene and daily self-care routines</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-4"><strong>1. to take a shower / a bath</strong> — принимать душ / ванну<br><em>Пример: I always take a warm shower before going to bed.</em><br><br><strong>2. to wash one's face and hands</strong> — умываться и мыть руки<br><em>Пример: Remember to wash your face and hands before eating.</em><br><br><strong>3. to brush one's teeth</strong> — чистить зубы<br><em>Пример: Dentists say you should brush your teeth twice a day.</em><br><br><strong>4. to dry oneself with a towel</strong> — вытираться полотенцем<br><em>Пример: After swimming in the pool, I dry myself with a big soft towel.</em><br><br><strong>5. to use soap and water</strong> — использовать мыло и воду<br><em>Пример: To kill germs, you must use soap and water when washing your hands.</em><br><br><strong>6. to apply sunscreen / cream</strong> — наносить солнцезащитный крем / крем для тела<br><em>Пример: Always apply sunscreen before you go out in the hot summer sun.</em><br><br><strong>7. to wash one's hair / use shampoo</strong> — мыть голову / использовать шампунь<br><em>Пример: I wash my hair every other day with a special shampoo.</em><br><br><strong>8. to comb / brush one's hair</strong> — расчёсывать волосы<br><em>Пример: She has very long hair and spends ten minutes brushing it every morning.</em><br><br><strong>9. to cut one's nails</strong> — стричь ногти<br><em>Пример: It's important to keep your hands clean and cut your nails regularly.</em><br><br><strong>10. to get a haircut</strong> — стричься (в парикмахерской)<br><em>Пример: My hair is too long, I need to get a haircut this weekend.</em><br><br><strong>11. to use deodorant</strong> — использовать дезодорант<br><em>Пример: Teenagers usually start to use deodorant when they go to secondary school.</em><br><br><strong>12. to keep oneself clean</strong> — поддерживать себя в чистоте<br><em>Пример: Keeping oneself clean is the best way to avoid getting sick.</em><br><br><strong>13. to change clothes / underwear regularly</strong> — регулярно менять одежду / нижнее бельё<br><em>Пример: You should change your sports clothes regularly so they don't smell bad.</em><br><br><strong>14. to take care of one's skin</strong> — ухаживать за кожей<br><em>Пример: Drinking lots of water helps to take care of your skin.</em><br><br><strong>15. to maintain personal hygiene</strong> — соблюдать личную гигиену<br><em>Пример: Our biology teacher gave us a lesson on how to maintain personal hygiene.</em></div></details></div></details></div></div>

!important;border-bottom:1px solid #66bb6a}.vocab-subcontent-2{padding:12px;background:#fff;line-height:1.6;font-size:13px;color:#444}.vocab-subitem-3{margin:8px 0;border:1px solid
