!important;list-style:none;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;font-size:14px;transition:all .3s}.vocab-subitem-4>summary:hover{background:#ba68c8 !important}.vocab-subitem-4>summary::-webkit-details-marker{display:none}.vocab-subitem-4>summary::after{content:"+";font-size:18px;color:#000;font-weight:bold}.vocab-subitem-4[open]>summary::after{content:"−"}.vocab-subitem-4[open]>summary{background:#ba68c8 !important;border-bottom:1px solid #ab47bc}.vocab-subcontent-4{padding:12px;background:#fff;line-height:1.6;font-size:13px;color:#444}</style><div class="vocab-accordion"><details class="vocab-item"><summary>Тема 10: Nature and Animals (Природа и животные)</summary><div class="vocab-content"><details class="vocab-subitem-1"><summary>Подтема 1: Wild and domestic animals, popular pets</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-1"><strong>1. to keep a pet</strong> — держать домашнее животное<br><em>Пример: I have always wanted to keep a pet, so my parents bought me a puppy.</em><br><br><strong>2. to walk the dog</strong> — выгуливать собаку<br><em>Пример: It is my daily responsibility to walk the dog in the park after school.</em><br><br><strong>3. to feed the pets</strong> — кормить питомцев<br><em>Пример: Don't forget to feed the pets, the cat and the parrot are very hungry!</em><br><br><strong>4. a wild animal</strong> — дикое животное<br><em>Пример: A wolf is a wild animal, so it is dangerous to approach it in the forest.</em><br><br><strong>5. a domestic animal</strong> — домашнее (сельскохозяйственное) животное<br><em>Пример: Cows, sheep, and horses are domestic animals that live on a farm.</em><br><br><strong>6. to tame an animal</strong> — приручить животное<br><em>Пример: It takes a lot of time and patience to tame a wild bird.</em><br><br><strong>7. a bird of prey</strong> — хищная птица<br><em>Пример: An eagle is a large bird of prey that hunts small animals.</em><br><br><strong>8. to hunt for food</strong> — охотиться за едой<br><em>Пример: Lions usually hunt for food at night when it is cooler.</em><br><br><strong>9. a herbivore / a carnivore</strong> — травоядное / плотоядное (хищник)<br><em>Пример: A rabbit is a herbivore, but a fox is a carnivore.</em><br><br><strong>10. to migrate south</strong> — мигрировать на юг<br><em>Пример: Many birds migrate south in autumn to find warmer weather.</em><br><br><strong>11. to hibernate in winter</strong> — впадать в спячку зимой<br><em>Пример: Bears eat a lot of berries in autumn because they hibernate in winter.</em><br><br><strong>12. to breed in captivity</strong> — размножаться в неволе (в зоопарке)<br><em>Пример: It is very difficult for some rare animals to breed in captivity.</em><br><br><strong>13. an endangered species</strong> — вымирающий вид<br><em>Пример: The Amur tiger is an endangered species, and we must protect it.</em><br><br><strong>14. to become extinct</strong> — вымереть<br><em>Пример: If we destroy the rainforests, many animals will become extinct.</em><br><br><strong>15. a furry / scaly / feathered friend</strong> — пушистый / чешуйчатый / пернатый друг<br><em>Пример: My cat is my favourite furry friend, she always purrs when I stroke her.</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-2"><summary>Подтема 2: Animal habitats (forest, farm, zoo, ocean)</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-2"><strong>1. natural habitat</strong> — естественная среда обитания<br><em>Пример: It is much better for animals to live in their natural habitat than in a zoo.</em><br><br><strong>2. to live in the wild</strong> — жить в дикой природе<br><em>Пример: Elephants live in the wild in Africa and Asia.</em><br><br><strong>3. a wildlife reserve / sanctuary</strong> — заповедник / убежище для диких животных<br><em>Пример: We visited a wildlife reserve where animals roam freely and safely.</em><br><br><strong>4. to roam the forest</strong> — бродить по лесу<br><em>Пример: Deer like to roam the forest in search of fresh grass and leaves.</em><br><br><strong>5. a coral reef</strong> — коралловый риф<br><em>Пример: A coral reef is home to thousands of colourful fish and sea turtles.</em><br><br><strong>6. deep ocean</strong> — глубины океана<br><em>Пример: Whales and sharks swim in the deep ocean.</em><br><br><strong>7. a farm yard</strong> — скотный двор<br><em>Пример: You can see chickens, ducks, and pigs in the farm yard.</em><br><br><strong>8. to graze in the meadow</strong> — пастись на лугу<br><em>Пример: Cows and sheep graze in the green meadow all summer long.</em><br><br><strong>9. a bird's nest</strong> — птичье гнездо<br><em>Пример: We found a little bird's nest with three blue eggs in the tree.</em><br><br><strong>10. a bear's den</strong> — берлога медведя<br><em>Пример: The bear sleeps in its den under the snow until spring comes.</em><br><br><strong>11. an underground burrow</strong> — подземная нора<br><em>Пример: Rabbits and foxes hide from predators in an underground burrow.</em><br><br><strong>12. to adapt to the environment</strong> — адаптироваться к окружающей среде<br><em>Пример: Polar bears have thick white fur to adapt to the cold environment.</em><br><br><strong>13. to camouflage</strong> — маскироваться<br><em>Пример: Chameleons can change their colour to camouflage and hide from enemies.</em><br><br><strong>14. a food chain</strong> — пищевая цепь<br><em>Пример: Plants, insects, frogs, and birds are all part of a food chain.</em><br><br><strong>15. at the top of the food chain</strong> — на вершине пищевой цепи<br><em>Пример: Lions and tigers are at the top of the food chain, they have no natural enemies.</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-3"><summary>Подтема 3: Landscapes, plants, trees, and flowers</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-3"><strong>1. beautiful scenery / landscape</strong> — красивый пейзаж / ландшафт<br><em>Пример: We took a lot of photos of the beautiful scenery in the mountains.</em><br><br><strong>2. a dense forest</strong> — густой лес<br><em>Пример: It is easy to get lost in a dense forest without a map or a compass.</em><br><br><strong>3. a steep mountain</strong> — крутая гора<br><em>Пример: Only experienced climbers can climb that steep mountain.</em><br><br><strong>4. a deep valley</strong> — глубокая долина<br><em>Пример: A fast river flows at the bottom of the deep valley.</em><br><br><strong>5. a flowing river</strong> — текущая река<br><em>Пример: We sat by the flowing river and watched the boats go by.</em><br><br><strong>6. to bloom / to blossom</strong> — цвести<br><em>Пример: Apple trees bloom in May, and the garden smells amazing.</em><br><br><strong>7. to plant a tree</strong> — посадить дерево<br><em>Пример: Our class decided to plant a tree in the school yard for Earth Day.</em><br><br><strong>8. to water the plants</strong> — поливать растения<br><em>Пример: If you forget to water the plants, they will dry out and die.</em><br><br><strong>9. to pick flowers</strong> — рвать цветы<br><em>Пример: Please don't pick flowers in the park, let them grow and make other people happy.</em><br><br><strong>10. a bunch of flowers</strong> — букет цветов<br><em>Пример: I bought a beautiful bunch of flowers for my mother's birthday.</em><br><br><strong>11. evergreen trees</strong> — вечнозеленые деревья<br><em>Пример: Pine and fir trees are evergreen trees, they stay green all winter.</em><br><br><strong>12. to lose leaves in autumn</strong> — терять листья осенью<br><em>Пример: Most trees lose their leaves in autumn and look bare until spring.</em><br><br><strong>13. to grow from a seed</strong> — вырасти из семени<br><em>Пример: Every giant oak tree starts to grow from a tiny seed.</em><br><br><strong>14. a weed</strong> — сорняк<br><em>Пример: My grandfather spends hours in the garden pulling out every weed.</em><br><br><strong>15. to harvest crops</strong> — собирать урожай<br><em>Пример: Farmers harvest crops like wheat and corn in early autumn.</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-4"><summary>Подтема 4: Nature conservation and basic ecological vocabulary</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-4"><strong>1. to protect the environment</strong> — защищать окружающую среду<br><em>Пример: It is our duty to protect the environment for future generations.</em><br><br><strong>2. nature conservation</strong> — охрана природы<br><em>Пример: Nature conservation helps to save rare animals and plants from disappearing.</em><br><br><strong>3. to pollute the air / water</strong> — загрязнять воздух / воду<br><em>Пример: Factories and cars pollute the air, which is bad for our health.</em><br><br><strong>4. environmental pollution</strong> — загрязнение окружающей среды<br><em>Пример: Environmental pollution is one of the biggest problems in the modern world.</em><br><br><strong>5. to recycle waste / rubbish</strong> — перерабатывать отходы / мусор<br><em>Пример: We should recycle waste like paper, glass, and plastic to save resources.</em><br><br><strong>6. to sort the garbage</strong> — сортировать мусор<br><em>Пример: My family likes to sort the garbage into different bins at home.</em><br><br><strong>7. a recycling bin</strong> — контейнер для переработки<br><em>Пример: Please throw your empty plastic bottle into the yellow recycling bin.</em><br><br><strong>8. to throw away / to litter</strong> — выбрасывать / мусорить (в неположенном месте)<br><em>Пример: It is terrible when people litter in the forest or on the beach.</em><br><br><strong>9. global warming</strong> — глобальное потепление<br><em>Пример: Global warming causes ice to melt in the Arctic, which is dangerous for polar bears.</em><br><br><strong>10. the greenhouse effect</strong> — парниковый эффект<br><em>Пример: Too much carbon dioxide in the atmosphere increases the greenhouse effect.</em><br><br><strong>11. to save energy / electricity</strong> — экономить энергию / электричество<br><em>Пример: Turn off the lights when you leave the room to save electricity.</em><br><br><strong>12. to waste water</strong> — тратить воду впустую<br><em>Пример: Don't leave the tap running while you brush your teeth, it's a waste of water.</em><br><br><strong>13. eco-friendly products</strong> — экологически чистые продукты / товары<br><em>Пример: More people are buying eco-friendly products that don't harm the planet.</em><br><br><strong>14. a reusable bag</strong> — многоразовая сумка (шоппер)<br><em>Пример: I always take a reusable bag to the supermarket instead of buying plastic ones.</em><br><br><strong>15. to reduce the carbon footprint</strong> — уменьшить углеродный след<br><em>Пример: We ride bicycles instead of driving cars to reduce our carbon footprint.</em></div></details></div></details><details class="vocab-item"><summary>Тема 11: Weather and Seasons (Погода и времена года)</summary><div class="vocab-content"><details class="vocab-subitem-1"><summary>Подтема 1: Seasons of the year and their characteristics</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-1"><strong>1. the four seasons</strong> — четыре времени года<br><em>Пример: The four seasons are spring, summer, autumn, and winter.</em><br><br><strong>2. in spring / in summer</strong> — весной / летом<br><em>Пример: I love to play outside in spring when the weather gets warmer.</em><br><br><strong>3. spring is coming</strong> — весна близко / наступает<br><em>Пример: The birds are singing more, spring is coming!</em><br><br><strong>4. nature wakes up</strong> — природа просыпается<br><em>Пример: In March, nature wakes up from the winter sleep.</em><br><br><strong>5. it gets warmer / colder</strong> — становится теплее / холоднее<br><em>Пример: It gets colder in November, so I need my warm jacket.</em><br><br><strong>6. the days are getting longer</strong> — дни становятся длиннее<br><em>Пример: In spring, the days are getting longer and the nights are shorter.</em><br><br><strong>7. the days are getting shorter</strong> — дни становятся короче<br><em>Пример: Autumn has arrived, and the days are getting shorter.</em><br><br><strong>8. leaves fall from trees</strong> — листья падают с деревьев<br><em>Пример: In autumn, the leaves fall from trees and cover the ground.</em><br><br><strong>9. the snow melts</strong> — снег тает<br><em>Пример: The snow melts quickly when the sun comes out in April.</em><br><br><strong>10. the weather changes</strong> — погода меняется<br><em>Пример: The weather changes very often in spring, so take an umbrella.</em><br><br><strong>11. a warm / mild winter</strong> — тёплая / мягкая зима<br><em>Пример: We had a very mild winter this year, there was almost no snow.</em><br><br><strong>12. a hot / scorching summer</strong> — жаркое / знойное лето<br><em>Пример: I hope we will have a hot summer so we can go to the beach.</em><br><br><strong>13. a rainy autumn</strong> — дождливая осень<br><em>Пример: It was a rainy autumn, we stayed at home and read books.</em><br><br><strong>14. early spring / late autumn</strong> — ранняя весна / поздняя осень<br><em>Пример: In early spring, you can see the first flowers in the forest.</em><br><br><strong>15. to enjoy the season</strong> — наслаждаться временем года<br><em>Пример: I try to enjoy every season, even cold winter.</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-2"><summary>Подтема 2: Weather phenomena (precipitation, wind, sun, thunderstorm, fog)</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-2"><strong>1. sunny weather</strong> — солнечная погода<br><em>Пример: It is sunny weather today, perfect for a picnic!</em><br><br><strong>2. cloudy / overcast sky</strong> — облачно / пасмурное небо<br><em>Пример: The sky is overcast, I think it is going to rain soon.</em><br><br><strong>3. to rain heavily / pour</strong> — идти сильному дождю / лить как из ведра<br><em>Пример: It rained heavily all night, and the streets were flooded.</em><br><br><strong>4. to snow</strong> — идти снегу<br><em>Пример: It started to snow in December, and everything turned white.</em><br><br><strong>5. to blow (wind)</strong> — дуть (ветер)<br><em>Пример: The wind blows strongly near the sea.</em><br><br><strong>6. strong wind / gale</strong> — сильный ветер / штормовой ветер<br><em>Пример: The strong wind blew my hat off my head.</em><br><br><strong>7. thunderstorm / lightning</strong> — гроза / молния<br><em>Пример: During the thunderstorm, we saw a bright flash of lightning.</em><br><br><strong>8. foggy weather</strong> — туманная погода<br><em>Пример: Drivers must be careful in foggy weather because visibility is poor.</em><br><br><strong>9. to be foggy</strong> — быть туманным<br><em>Пример: It is foggy this morning, I can barely see the house across the street.</em><br><br><strong>10. hail</strong> — град<br><em>Пример: Suddenly, it started to hail, and the ground was covered with ice balls.</em><br><br><strong>11. rainbow</strong> — радуга<br><em>Пример: After the rain, a beautiful rainbow appeared in the sky.</em><br><br><strong>12. drizzle</strong> — морось (мелкий дождь)<br><em>Пример: It was just a drizzle, so we didn't open our umbrellas.</em><br><br><strong>13. shower</strong> — ливень (кратковременный дождь)<br><em>Пример: We got caught in a sudden shower and ran to the bus stop.</em><br><br><strong>14. weather conditions</strong> — погодные условия<br><em>Пример: Bad weather conditions can cause traffic jams on the roads.</em><br><br><strong>15. bad weather</strong> — плохая погода<br><em>Пример: There is no such thing as bad weather, only bad clothes!</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-3"><summary>Подтема 3: Air temperature and weather forecasts</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-3"><strong>1. weather forecast</strong> — прогноз погоды<br><em>Пример: My mum always checks the weather forecast before going out.</em><br><br><strong>2. air temperature</strong> — температура воздуха<br><em>Пример: The air temperature is 20 degrees today.</em><br><br><strong>3. above / below zero</strong> — выше / ниже нуля<br><em>Пример: The temperature was below zero last night, so the roads are icy.</em><br><br><strong>4. degrees Celsius</strong> — градусы Цельсия<br><em>Пример: It is 25 degrees Celsius outside, let's go swimming!</em><br><br><strong>5. to check the weather</strong> — проверять погоду<br><em>Пример: I check the weather on my phone every morning.</em><br><br><strong>6. the forecast says</strong> — прогноз говорит<br><em>Пример: The forecast says it will be windy tomorrow.</em><br><br><strong>7. to expect rain / snow</strong> — ожидается дождь / снег<br><em>Пример: We expect heavy rain in the evening, so stay indoors.</em><br><br><strong>8. a drop in temperature</strong> — падение температуры<br><em>Пример: A drop in temperature is expected this weekend.</em><br><br><strong>9. heatwave</strong> — жара / аномальная жара<br><em>Пример: During the heatwave, it is important to drink lots of water.</em><br><br><strong>10. frost</strong> — заморозки / мороз<br><em>Пример: There was a hard frost last night, and the car windows were covered with ice.</em><br><br><strong>11. mild winter</strong> — мягкая зима<br><em>Пример: In Europe, winters are often mild with very little snow.</em><br><br><strong>12. freezing cold</strong> — морозно / очень холодно<br><em>Пример: Put on your gloves, it is freezing cold outside!</em><br><br><strong>13. boiling hot</strong> — очень жарко<br><em>Пример: I can't walk anywhere when it is boiling hot.</em><br><br><strong>14. humid</strong> — влажный (о воздухе)<br><em>Пример: The weather is hot and humid, it is hard to breathe.</em><br><br><strong>15. to dress according to the weather</strong> — одеваться по погоде<br><em>Пример: Always dress according to the weather so you don't catch a cold.</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-4"><summary>Подтема 4: Favorite seasons and typical seasonal activities</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-4"><strong>1. favorite season</strong> — любимое время года<br><em>Пример: My favorite season is summer because I don't have to go to school.</em><br><br><strong>2. to enjoy summer</strong> — наслаждаться летом<br><em>Пример: I enjoy summer because I can spend a lot of time outdoors.</em><br><br><strong>3. to go swimming in the river</strong> — купаться в реке<br><em>Пример: In July, we go swimming in the river every day.</em><br><br><strong>4. to sunbathe</strong> — загорать<br><em>Пример: She likes to sunbathe on the beach while listening to music.</em><br><br><strong>5. to go skiing / snowboarding</strong> — кататься на лыжах / сноуборде<br><em>Пример: My family goes skiing in the mountains every winter holiday.</em><br><br><strong>6. to build a snowman</strong> — лепить снеговика<br><em>Пример: The children built a snowman and put a carrot on its nose.</em><br><br><strong>7. to have snowball fights</strong> — играть в снежки<br><em>Пример: We had a great snowball fight in the park yesterday.</em><br><br><strong>8. to go for a picnic</strong> — идти на пикник<br><em>Пример: Let's go for a picnic in the forest this Sunday!</em><br><br><strong>9. to pick berries / mushrooms</strong> — собирать ягоды / грибы<br><em>Пример: My grandmother likes to pick berries in the forest in August.</em><br><br><strong>10. to fly a kite</strong> — запускать воздушного змея<br><em>Пример: It is windy today, let's fly a kite in the park!</em><br><br><strong>11. to admire the autumn leaves</strong> — любоваться осенними листьями<br><em>Пример: We walked in the park to admire the beautiful autumn leaves.</em><br><br><strong>12. to plant flowers / trees</strong> — сажать цветы / деревья<br><em>Пример: In spring, my dad helps me to plant flowers in the garden.</em><br><br><strong>13. to harvest crops</strong> — собирать урожай<br><em>Пример: Farmers harvest crops in autumn, it is a busy time for them.</em><br><br><strong>14. seasonal clothes</strong> — сезонная одежда<br><em>Пример: You need to change your seasonal clothes when winter comes.</em><br><br><strong>15. to spend time indoors / outdoors</strong> — проводить время в помещении / на свежем воздухе<br><em>Пример: When it rains, I spend time indoors, but when it's sunny, I go outdoors.</em></div></details></div></details><details class="vocab-item"><summary>Тема 12: Travel, City, and Transport (Путешествия, город и транспорт)</summary><div class="vocab-content"><details class="vocab-subitem-1"><summary>Подтема 1: Types of transport and buying tickets</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-1"><strong>1. public transport</strong> — общественный транспорт<br><em>Пример: I prefer using public transport because it is cheaper than taking a taxi.</em><br><br><strong>2. to catch a bus / train</strong> — успеть на автобус / поезд<br><em>Пример: We ran to the stop to catch a bus, but it left without us.</em><br><br><strong>3. to miss a bus / flight</strong> — опоздать на автобус / рейс<br><em>Пример: We woke up late and missed our flight to London.</em><br><br><strong>4. to get on / get off</strong> — садиться / выходить (из автобуса, поезда, самолёта)<br><em>Пример: Let's get off at the next station and walk to the park.</em><br><br><strong>5. to get in / get out of</strong> — садиться / выходить (из машины, такси)<br><em>Пример: My dad stopped the car and told us to get out of it carefully.</em><br><br><strong>6. a single / return ticket</strong> — билет в один конец / туда и обратно<br><em>Пример: I need a return ticket to Paris, please, coming back on Sunday.</em><br><br><strong>7. a travel card / season ticket</strong> — проездной билет<br><em>Пример: It is cheaper to buy a monthly travel card if you use the underground every day.</em><br><br><strong>8. to validate a ticket</strong> — прокомпостировать билет<br><em>Пример: Don't forget to validate your ticket when you get on the tram.</em><br><br><strong>9. a ticket inspector</strong> — контролёр в транспорте<br><em>Пример: The ticket inspector asked to see my travel card.</em><br><br><strong>10. to be stuck in a traffic jam</strong> — застрять в пробке<br><em>Пример: We were late for school because the school bus was stuck in a traffic jam.</em><br><br><strong>11. to give someone a lift</strong> — подвезти кого-либо<br><em>Пример: My neighbour gave me a lift to the shopping centre yesterday.</em><br><br><strong>12. a bus stop / a bus station</strong> — автобусная остановка / автостанция<br><em>Пример: The main bus station is located near the railway station.</em><br><br><strong>13. a railway station</strong> — железнодорожный вокзал<br><em>Пример: We arrived at the railway station an hour before the train departed.</em><br><br><strong>14. a departure lounge</strong> — зал ожидания (в аэропорту)<br><em>Пример: We sat in the departure lounge and drank coffee before boarding the plane.</em><br><br><strong>15. to board a plane / ship</strong> — садиться на борт самолёта / корабля<br><em>Пример: Passengers are invited to board the plane through gate number five.</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-2"><summary>Подтема 2: City infrastructure and asking for directions</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-2"><strong>1. city centre / downtown</strong> — центр города<br><em>Пример: Most of the famous museums and monuments are in the city centre.</em><br><br><strong>2. a residential area</strong> — жилой район<br><em>Пример: I live in a quiet residential area, far from the noisy streets.</em><br><br><strong>3. a pedestrian crossing</strong> — пешеходный переход<br><em>Пример: You must only cross the road at the pedestrian crossing.</em><br><br><strong>4. a traffic light</strong> — светофор<br><em>Пример: Wait until the traffic light turns green before you cross the street.</em><br><br><strong>5. a roundabout</strong> — кольцевая развязка<br><em>Пример: Take the second exit at the roundabout to get to the hospital.</em><br><br><strong>6. a pavement / sidewalk</strong> — тротуар<br><em>Пример: Please walk on the pavement, it is dangerous to walk on the road.</em><br><br><strong>7. to ask for directions</strong> — спрашивать дорогу<br><em>Пример: If you don't know the way, just ask for directions.</em><br><br><strong>8. to give directions</strong> — объяснять дорогу<br><em>Пример: The police officer was very polite and gave us clear directions.</em><br><br><strong>9. to get lost</strong> — заблудиться<br><em>Пример: We got lost in the old town because we didn't have a map.</em><br><br><strong>10. to turn left / right at the corner</strong> — повернуть налево / направо на углу<br><em>Пример: Turn right at the corner and the pharmacy will be on your left.</em><br><br><strong>11. to go straight ahead</strong> — идти прямо<br><em>Пример: Go straight ahead for two blocks, and you will see the cinema.</em><br><br><strong>12. to cross the street</strong> — переходить улицу<br><em>Пример: Look both ways before you cross the street.</em><br><br><strong>13. opposite / next to / between</strong> — напротив / рядом с / между<br><em>Пример: The bakery is opposite the bank and next to the flower shop.</em><br><br><strong>14. a town hall</strong> — ратуша, мэрия<br><em>Пример: The town hall is a beautiful old building in the main square.</em><br><br><strong>15. a shopping mall / department store</strong> — торговый центр / универмаг<br><em>Пример: We spent the whole afternoon walking around the huge shopping mall.</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-3"><summary>Подтема 3: Travelling, holidays, and accommodation options</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-3"><strong>1. to go on holiday / vacation</strong> — поехать в отпуск / на каникулы<br><em>Пример: My family is going on holiday to the seaside next month.</em><br><br><strong>2. to pack a suitcase</strong> — собирать чемодан<br><em>Пример: I always pack my suitcase the night before the trip.</em><br><br><strong>3. to travel light</strong> — путешествовать налегке<br><em>Пример: I only take a small backpack because I prefer to travel light.</em><br><br><strong>4. a package holiday / tour</strong> — пакетный тур<br><em>Пример: We bought a package holiday to Turkey, so the hotel and flights are included.</em><br><br><strong>5. to book a hotel room</strong> — забронировать номер в отеле<br><em>Пример: You should book a hotel room in advance during the summer season.</em><br><br><strong>6. to check in / check out</strong> — заселяться / выселяться (из отеля)<br><em>Пример: We need to check out of the hotel by 12 o'clock midday.</em><br><br><strong>7. a youth hostel</strong> — молодёжный хостел<br><em>Пример: Staying in a youth hostel is a great way to meet other travellers.</em><br><br><strong>8. to go camping</strong> — ходить в поход с палатками<br><em>Пример: We love to go camping in the forest and sleep under the stars.</em><br><br><strong>9. to pitch a tent</strong> — разбить палатку<br><em>Пример: My dad knows how to pitch a tent very quickly.</em><br><br><strong>10. to go sightseeing</strong> — осматривать достопримечательности<br><em>Пример: When we visit a new city, we always go sightseeing on the first day.</em><br><br><strong>11. a guided tour</strong> — экскурсия с гидом<br><em>Пример: We took a guided tour of the ancient castle and learned a lot of history.</em><br><br><strong>12. to buy souvenirs</strong> — покупать сувениры<br><em>Пример: I bought some nice souvenirs for my friends at the gift shop.</em><br><br><strong>13. a travel agency</strong> — туристическое агентство<br><em>Пример: The travel agency helped us plan our trip to Italy.</em><br><br><strong>14. a destination</strong> — пункт назначения<br><em>Пример: Paris is a very popular tourist destination all year round.</em><br><br><strong>15. a return flight</strong> — обратный рейс<br><em>Пример: Our return flight is delayed by two hours due to bad weather.</em></div></details><details class="vocab-subitem-4"><summary>Подтема 4: Countries, capitals, continents, and nationalities</summary><div class="vocab-subcontent-4"><strong>1. a continent</strong> — континент, материк<br><em>Пример: Australia is the smallest continent in the world.</em><br><br><strong>2. a capital city</strong> — столица<br><em>Пример: Canberra is the capital city of Australia, not Sydney.</em><br><br><strong>3. a native speaker</strong> — носитель языка<br><em>Пример: The best way to improve your English is to speak with a native speaker.</em><br><br><strong>4. a foreigner</strong> — иностранец<br><em>Пример: As a foreigner, I sometimes find it hard to understand the local slang.</em><br><br><strong>5. a border</strong> — граница<br><em>Пример: The river forms a natural border between the two countries.</em><br><br><strong>6. to cross the border</strong> — пересекать границу<br><em>Пример: You need a valid passport to cross the border.</em><br><br><strong>7. a passport / visa</strong> — паспорт / виза<br><em>Пример: My passport expires next month, so I need to renew it.</em><br><br><strong>8. a customs officer</strong> — таможенник<br><em>Пример: The customs officer asked me to open my suitcase.</em><br><br><strong>9. to declare goods</strong> — декларировать товары<br><em>Пример: If you are carrying a lot of cash, you must declare it at customs.</em><br><br><strong>10. a citizen</strong> — гражданин<br><em>Пример: He has lived in Canada for ten years and is now a Canadian citizen.</em><br><br><strong>11. nationality</strong> — национальность, гражданство<br><em>Пример: The form asks for your name, date of birth, and nationality.</em><br><br><strong>12. to travel abroad</strong> — путешествовать за границу<br><em>Пример: Many students dream of travelling abroad to study at a foreign university.</em><br><br><strong>13. a multinational country</strong> — многонациональная страна<br><em>Пример: Russia is a huge multinational country with many different cultures.</em><br><br><strong>14. a cultural exchange</strong> — культурный обмен<br><em>Пример: I took part in a cultural exchange programme and lived with a host family in the UK.</em><br><br><strong>15. to speak a foreign language</strong> — говорить на иностранном языке<br><em>Пример: It is very useful to speak a foreign language when you travel the world.</em></div></details></div></details></div></div>

<!-- ================= ТЕМА 1.1 ================= -->

Members and Relatives 👪</h2><button class="dr-btn" id="btn-learn-t1p1" style="background:#f59e0b;color:#fff" onclick="togglePanel('learn-t1p1')">📘 Learn</button><div class="dr-panel" id="learn-t1p1"><div class="ex-card"><h4>Examples & Practice 💬</h4><div class="ex-item">1) I live in a <span class="vt-word">nuclear family<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>nuclear family</b><br>нуклеарная семья (родители и дети)</span></span> with my parents and my sister. 👨‍‍👧</div><div class="ex-item">2) We usually spend summer holidays with our <span class="vt-word">extended family<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>extended family</b><br>расширенная семья</span></span>. 👪</div><div class="ex-item">3) I don't have any brothers or sisters, I am <span class="vt-word">an only child<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>an only child</b><br>единственный ребёнок в семье</span></span>. 🧍</div><div class="ex-item">4) <span class="vt-word">The eldest<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>the eldest child</b><br>старший ребёнок</span></span> child often helps parents, and <span class="vt-word">the youngest<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>the youngest child</b><br>младший ребёнок</span></span> child plays all day. 👦</div><div class="ex-item">5) Being <span class="vt-word">a middle child<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>a middle child</b><br>средний ребёнок</span></span>, he knows how to share and compromise. 🧒</div><div class="ex-item">6) My cousins are <span class="vt-word">twins<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>twins</b><br>близнецы / двойняшки</span></span>, they look exactly the same. 👯</div><div class="ex-item">7) A <span class="vt-word">distant relative<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>a distant relative</b><br>дальний родственник</span></span> invited us to his wedding last month. 💒</div><div class="ex-item">8) Only <span class="vt-word">close relatives<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>close relatives</b><br>близкие родственники</span></span> came to our small family dinner. 🍽️</div><div class="ex-item">9) Are you <span class="vt-word">related to<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>to be related to</b><br>быть в родстве с кем-либо</span></span> the Smiths? My grandmother <span class="vt-word">on my mother's side<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>on my mother's side</b><br>по материнской линии</span></span> is their neighbour! </div><div class="ex-item">10) My older brother wants to <span class="vt-word">start a family<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>to start a family</b><br>создать семью, завести детей</span></span> next year. 💍</div><div class="ex-item">11) It is not easy to <span class="vt-word">bring up<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>to bring up children</b><br>воспитывать детей</span></span> children, but I <span class="vt-word">grew up in a big family<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>to grow up in a big family</b><br>расти в большой семье</span></span>, and my parents know how. 🧸</div><div class="ex-item">12) We drew our <span class="vt-word">family tree<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>family tree</b><br>генеалогическое древо</span></span> for the history project at school. 🌳</div></div><div class="practice-box"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: My Family Tree 🌳</h4><div class="practice-text">Let me show you my <span class="blur-wrapper" id="bw-t1p1-1" onclick="toggleBlur('bw-t1p1-1')"><span class="blur-text">family tree 🌳</span><span class="tooltip"> генеалогическое древо</span></span>! I <span class="blur-wrapper" id="bw-t1p1-2" onclick="toggleBlur('bw-t1p1-2')"><span class="blur-text">grew up in a big family 🏡</span><span class="tooltip"> вырос в большой семье</span></span>, so I am used to noise and fun. My parents, my sister and I are a <span class="blur-wrapper" id="bw-t1p1-3" onclick="toggleBlur('bw-t1p1-3')"><span class="blur-text">nuclear family 👨‍👩‍👧</span><span class="tooltip"> нуклеарная семья</span></span>, but at holidays our <span class="blur-wrapper" id="bw-t1p1-4" onclick="toggleBlur('bw-t1p1-4')"><span class="blur-text">extended family 👪</span><span class="tooltip"> расширенная семья</span></span> gets together. My cousin Max is <span class="blur-wrapper" id="bw-t1p1-5" onclick="toggleBlur('bw-t1p1-5')"><span class="blur-text">an only child 🧍</span><span class="tooltip"> единственный ребёнок</span></span>, and his <span class="blur-wrapper" id="bw-t1p1-6" onclick="toggleBlur('bw-t1p1-6')"><span class="blur-text">close relatives ❤️</span><span class="tooltip"> близкие родственники</span></span> live far away. A <span class="blur-wrapper" id="bw-t1p1-7" onclick="toggleBlur('bw-t1p1-7')"><span class="blur-text">distant relative 🧔</span><span class="tooltip"> дальний родственник</span></span> of ours lives in Canada. My grandmother <span class="blur-wrapper" id="bw-t1p1-8" onclick="toggleBlur('bw-t1p1-8')"><span class="blur-text">on my mother's side 🥧</span><span class="tooltip"> по материнской линии</span></span> bakes the best pies. We are <span class="blur-wrapper" id="bw-t1p1-9" onclick="toggleBlur('bw-t1p1-9')"><span class="blur-text">related to 🔗</span><span class="tooltip"> в родстве с</span></span> the Browns: our grandfathers were brothers. My aunt and uncle know how to <span class="blur-wrapper" id="bw-t1p1-10" onclick="toggleBlur('bw-t1p1-10')"><span class="blur-text">bring up 🧸</span><span class="tooltip"> воспитывать</span></span> children well. My older brother wants to <span class="blur-wrapper" id="bw-t1p1-11" onclick="toggleBlur('bw-t1p1-11')"><span class="blur-text">start a family 💍</span><span class="tooltip"> создать семью</span></span> next year. In our family, <span class="blur-wrapper" id="bw-t1p1-12" onclick="toggleBlur('bw-t1p1-12')"><span class="blur-text">the eldest 👦</span><span class="tooltip"> старший</span></span> child cooks dinner and <span class="blur-wrapper" id="bw-t1p1-13" onclick="toggleBlur('bw-t1p1-13')"><span class="blur-text">the youngest 👶</span><span class="tooltip"> младший</span></span> child sets the table. My cousins are <span class="blur-wrapper" id="bw-t1p1-14" onclick="toggleBlur('bw-t1p1-14')"><span class="blur-text">twins 👯</span><span class="tooltip"> близнецы</span></span>, and I am <span class="blur-wrapper" id="bw-t1p1-15" onclick="toggleBlur('bw-t1p1-15')"><span class="blur-text">a middle child 🧒</span><span class="tooltip"> средний ребёнок</span></span> — I always help everyone make peace!</div><button class="practice-reset-btn" onclick="resetPracticeText('lesson-t1p1')">reset</button></div></div><button class="dr-btn test" id="btn-test-t1p1" style="background:#ef4444;color:#fff" onclick="togglePanel('test-t1p1')"> Test</button><div class="dr-panel" id="test-t1p1"><h3 class="qz-title" id="test-title-t1p1">👪 Test: Family Members and Relatives</h3><p class="qz-sub">Choose the correct option (15 questions — one per phrase):</p><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">1</div><div class="qz-sent">I live with my parents and my sister. We are a ___.</div><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q1" id="t1p1_q1a" class="qz-r corr" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q1a" class="qz-opt">A) nuclear family</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q1" id="t1p1_q1b" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q1_fb_b" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q1b" class="qz-opt">B) distant relative</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q1" id="t1p1_q1c" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q1_fb_c" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q1c" class="qz-opt">C) family tree</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Nuclear family</b> = нуклеарная семья (родители и дети).</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q1_fb_b">❌ <b>Distant relative</b> = дальний родственник. Это не подходит, потому что в предложении говорится о семье, в которой вы живёте, а не о родственниках.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q1_fb_c">❌ <b>Family tree</b> = генеалогическое древо. Это схема ваших родственников, а не тип семьи.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">2</div><div class="qz-sent">At Christmas all our aunts, uncles and cousins come to us. Our ___ gets together.</div><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q2" id="t1p1_q2a" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q2_fb_a" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q2a" class="qz-opt">A) only child</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q2" id="t1p1_q2b" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q2_fb_b" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q2b" class="qz-opt">B) middle child</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q2" id="t1p1_q2c" class="qz-r corr" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q2c" class="qz-opt">C) extended family</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Extended family</b> = расширенная семья.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q2_fb_a">❌ <b>Only child</b> = единственный ребёнок. Это описывает одного ребёнка без братьев/сестёр, а не группу родственников.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q2_fb_b">❌ <b>Middle child</b> = средний ребёнок. Это описывает позицию ребёнка в семье, а не всю семью.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">3</div><div class="qz-sent">Tom has no brothers or sisters. He is ___.</div><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q3" id="t1p1_q3a" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q3_fb_a" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q3a" class="qz-opt">A) a twin</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q3" id="t1p1_q3b" class="qz-r corr" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q3b" class="qz-opt">B) an only child</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q3" id="t1p1_q3c" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q3_fb_c" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q3c" class="qz-opt">C) the eldest child</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>An only child</b> = единственный ребёнок в семье.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q3_fb_a">❌ <b>A twin</b> = близнец. Близнец всегда имеет брата или сестру, рождённых в тот же день, поэтому этот вариант противоречит условию.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q3_fb_c">❌ <b>The eldest child</b> = старший ребёнок. Старший ребёнок подразумевает, что есть младшие, но в условии сказано, что у Тома нет братьев или сестёр.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">4</div><div class="qz-sent">My brother Nick is 15 and I am 12, so Nick is ___ child in our family.</div><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q4" id="t1p1_q4a" class="qz-r corr" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q4a" class="qz-opt">A) the eldest</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q4" id="t1p1_q4b" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q4_fb_b" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q4b" class="qz-opt">B) the youngest</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q4" id="t1p1_q4c" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q4_fb_c" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q4c" class="qz-opt">C) a middle</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>The eldest child</b> = старший ребёнок.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q4_fb_b">❌ <b>The youngest</b> = младший. Ник старше (15 лет), поэтому он не может быть младшим ребёнком.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q4_fb_c">❌ <b>A middle child</b> = средний ребёнок. Средний ребёнок находится между старшим и младшим, но Ник — самый старший из двух братьев.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">5</div><div class="qz-sent">My little sister is only five. She is ___ child in our family.</div><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q5" id="t1p1_q5a" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q5_fb_a" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q5a" class="qz-opt">A) the eldest</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q5" id="t1p1_q5b" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q5_fb_b" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q5b" class="qz-opt">B) a distant</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q5" id="t1p1_q5c" class="qz-r corr" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q5c" class="qz-opt">C) the youngest</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>The youngest child</b> = младший ребёнок.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q5_fb_a">❌ <b>The eldest</b> = старший. Сестре всего 5 лет, она самая младшая в семье, а не старшая.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q5_fb_b">❌ <b>A distant</b> = дальний. "Distant child" не существует в английском языке. Distant используется только с relative (дальний родственник).</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">6</div><div class="qz-sent">I am not the oldest and not the youngest. I am ___.</div><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q6" id="t1p1_q6a" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q6_fb_a" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q6a" class="qz-opt">A) a twin</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q6" id="t1p1_q6b" class="qz-r corr" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q6b" class="qz-opt">B) a middle child</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q6" id="t1p1_q6c" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q6_fb_c" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q6c" class="qz-opt">C) an only child</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>A middle child</b> = средний ребёнок.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q6_fb_a">❌ <b>A twin</b> = близнец. Близнецы обычно либо старшие, либо младшие, либо средние — это не описывает позицию "между старшим и младшим".</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q6_fb_c">❌ <b>An only child</b> = единственный ребёнок. Единственный ребёнок не может быть ни старшим, ни младшим, потому что он один в семье.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">7</div><div class="qz-sent">Tom and Tim were born on the same day. They are ___.</div><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q7" id="t1p1_q7a" class="qz-r corr" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q7a" class="qz-opt">A) twins</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q7" id="t1p1_q7b" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q7_fb_b" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q7b" class="qz-opt">B) distant relatives</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q7" id="t1p1_q7c" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q7_fb_c" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q7c" class="qz-opt">C) middle children</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Twins</b> = близнецы / двойняшки.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q7_fb_b">❌ <b>Distant relatives</b> = дальние родственники. Дальние родственники не обязательно рождаются в один день — это просто родственники, с которыми мало общаются.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q7_fb_c">❌ <b>Middle children</b> = средние дети. Средние дети — это те, кто находится между старшим и младшим, но это не связано с датой рождения.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">8</div><div class="qz-sent">We see him very rarely, but a ___ invited us to his wedding.</div><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q8" id="t1p1_q8a" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q8_fb_a" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q8a" class="qz-opt">A) close relative</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q8" id="t1p1_q8b" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q8_fb_b" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q8b" class="qz-opt">B) nuclear family</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q8" id="t1p1_q8c" class="qz-r corr" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q8c" class="qz-opt">C) distant relative</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>A distant relative</b> = дальний родственник.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q8_fb_a">❌ <b>A close relative</b> = близкий родственник. Близких родственников мы видим часто, а в предложении сказано "very rarely" (очень редко).</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q8_fb_b">❌ <b>Nuclear family</b> = нуклеарная семья. Это тип семьи (родители и дети), а не конкретный человек, который может пригласить на свадьбу.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">9</div><div class="qz-sent">Only our ___ came to the small family dinner: grandparents, aunts and uncles.</div><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q9" id="t1p1_q9a" class="qz-r corr" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q9a" class="qz-opt">A) close relatives</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q9" id="t1p1_q9b" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q9_fb_b" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q9b" class="qz-opt">B) distant relatives</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q9" id="t1p1_q9c" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q9_fb_c" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q9c" class="qz-opt">C) twins</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Close relatives</b> = близкие родственники.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q9_fb_b">❌ <b>Distant relatives</b> = дальние родственники. Бабушки, дедушки, тёти и дяди — это близкие родственники, а не дальние.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q9_fb_c">❌ <b>Twins</b> = близнецы. В списке перечислены разные родственники (бабушки, тёти, дяди), а не только близнецы.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">10</div><div class="qz-sent">Are you ___ the Smiths? Your grandfathers are brothers!</div><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q10" id="t1p1_q10a" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q10_fb_a" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q10a" class="qz-opt">A) married to</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q10" id="t1p1_q10b" class="qz-r corr" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q10b" class="qz-opt">B) related to</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q10" id="t1p1_q10c" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q10_fb_c" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q10c" class="qz-opt">C) keen on</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>To be related to</b> = быть в родстве с кем-либо.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q10_fb_a">❌ <b>Married to</b> = женат на / замужем за. Это означает брак, а не кровное родство через дедушек.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q10_fb_c">❌ <b>Keen on</b> = увлекаться чем-то / интересоваться. Это выражение используется для хобби и интересов, а не для семейных связей.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">11</div><div class="qz-sent">My grandmother ___ is my mum's mother. She bakes delicious pies.</div><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q11" id="t1p1_q11a" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q11_fb_a" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q11a" class="qz-opt">A) on my father's side</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q11" id="t1p1_q11b" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q11_fb_b" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q11b" class="qz-opt">B) in a big family</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q11" id="t1p1_q11c" class="qz-r corr" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q11c" class="qz-opt">C) on my mother's side</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>On my mother's side</b> = по материнской линии.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q11_fb_a">❌ <b>On my father's side</b> = по отцовской линии. В предложении сказано "my mum's mother" (мамина мама), значит это материнская сторона.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q11_fb_b">❌ <b>In a big family</b> = в большой семье. Это описывает размер семьи, а не то, по какой линии приходится бабушка.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">12</div><div class="qz-sent">My older brother wants to ___ next year and have two children.</div><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q12" id="t1p1_q12a" class="qz-r corr" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q12a" class="qz-opt">A) start a family</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q12" id="t1p1_q12b" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q12_fb_b" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q12b" class="qz-opt">B) draw a family tree</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q12" id="t1p1_q12c" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q12_fb_c" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q12c" class="qz-opt">C) be a middle child</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>To start a family</b> = создать семью, завести детей.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q12_fb_b">❌ <b>To draw a family tree</b> = нарисовать генеалогическое древо. Это не связано с желанием иметь детей — это просто рисование схемы.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q12_fb_c">❌ <b>To be a middle child</b> = быть средним ребёнком. Позицию в семье нельзя выбрать — ты либо средний, либо нет, это не действие.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">13</div><div class="qz-sent">My parents are kind but strict. They know how to ___ children.</div><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q13" id="t1p1_q13a" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q13_fb_a" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q13a" class="qz-opt">A) grow up</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q13" id="t1p1_q13b" class="qz-r corr" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q13b" class="qz-opt">B) bring up</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q13" id="t1p1_q13c" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q13_fb_c" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q13c" class="qz-opt">C) get together</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>To bring up children</b> = воспитывать детей.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q13_fb_a">❌ <b>To grow up</b> = расти, взрослеть. Это то, что делают дети, а не родители. Родители "bring up" (воспитывают), а дети "grow up" (растут).</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q13_fb_c">❌ <b>To get together</b> = собраться вместе. Это означает встречу людей, а не воспитание детей.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">14</div><div class="qz-sent">I ___, so I am used to noise and fun.</div><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q14" id="t1p1_q14a" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q14_fb_a" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q14a" class="qz-opt">A) started a family</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q14" id="t1p1_q14b" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q14_fb_b" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q14b" class="qz-opt">B) am an only child</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q14" id="t1p1_q14c" class="qz-r corr" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q14c" class="qz-opt">C) grew up in a big family</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>To grow up in a big family</b> = расти в большой семье.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q14_fb_a">❌ <b>Started a family</b> = создал семью (завёл детей). Это не объясняет, почему человек привык к шуму — создание семьи не делает тебя привычным к шуму.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q14_fb_b">❌ <b>Am an only child</b> = единственный ребёнок. Единственные дети обычно растут в тишине, а не в шуме и веселье.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">15</div><div class="qz-sent">We drew our ___ for the history project: all our relatives since 1900!</div><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q15" id="t1p1_q15a" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q15_fb_a" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q15a" class="qz-opt">A) nuclear family</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q15" id="t1p1_q15b" class="qz-r corr" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q15b" class="qz-opt">B) family tree</label><input type="radio" name="t1p1_q15" id="t1p1_q15c" class="qz-r wrng" data-feedback="t1p1_q15_fb_c" onchange="showFeedback(this);updateTestStats('t1p1');if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress()"><label for="t1p1_q15c" class="qz-opt">C) middle child</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Family tree</b> = генеалогическое древо.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q15_fb_a">❌ <b>Nuclear family</b> = нуклеарная семья. Нуклеарная семья — это только родители и дети, а не все родственники с 1900 года.</div><div class="qz-fb no" id="t1p1_q15_fb_c">❌ <b>Middle child</b> = средний ребёнок. Нельзя нарисовать "среднего ребёнка" как проект — это описание позиции в семье, а не схема.</div></div><div class="qz-stats" id="stats-t1p1">Результат: 0 из 15 (0%)</div><button class="task-reset-btn" onclick="resetTest('t1p1')">reset</button></div></div></div><script>function togglePanel(id){const p=document.getElementById(id);p.classList.toggle('active');saveTaskState(p);if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress();}function toggleBlur(id){const el=document.getElementById(id);el.classList.toggle('revealed');saveTaskState(el);if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress();}function saveTaskState(el){const pageKey='eng_lesson_'+window.location.pathname;const elId=el.id;if(!elId)return;let value=el.classList.contains('active')||el.classList.contains('revealed');localStorage.setItem(pageKey+'_'+elId,JSON.stringify(value));}function restoreTaskState(){const pageKey='eng_lesson_'+window.location.pathname;document.querySelectorAll('.dr-panel,.blur-wrapper').forEach(el=>{const elId=el.id;if(!elId)return;const saved=localStorage.getItem(pageKey+'_'+elId);if(saved!==null){const value=JSON.parse(saved);if(value){el.classList.add(el.classList.contains('blur-wrapper')?'revealed':'active');}}});const wraps=document.querySelectorAll('.dr-wrap');wraps.forEach(w=>{const prefix=w.id.replace('lesson-','');updateTestStats(prefix);restoreTestFeedback(prefix);});}function restoreTestFeedback(prefix){const panel=document.getElementById('test-'+prefix);if(!panel)return;panel.querySelectorAll('input[type="radio"]:checked').forEach(r=>{showFeedback(r);});}function showFeedback(radio){const card=radio.closest('.qz-card');if(!card)return;card.querySelectorAll('.qz-fb.no').forEach(fb=>fb.classList.remove('visible'));if(radio.classList.contains('corr')){return;}const feedbackId=radio.getAttribute('data-feedback');if(feedbackId){const fb=document.getElementById(feedbackId);if(fb)fb.classList.add('visible');}}function resetTest(prefix){if(!confirm('Сбросить все ответы в этом тесте?'))return;const panel=document.getElementById('test-'+prefix);if(!panel)return;panel.querySelectorAll('input[type="radio"]').forEach(r=>r.checked=false);panel.querySelectorAll('.qz-fb.no').forEach(fb=>fb.classList.remove('visible'));updateTestStats(prefix);if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress();const title=document.getElementById('test-title-'+prefix);if(title){title.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'start'});}}function resetPracticeText(wrapId){if(!confirm('Сбросить все раскрытые слова в Practice Text?'))return;const wrap=document.getElementById(wrapId);if(!wrap)return;wrap.querySelectorAll('.blur-wrapper.revealed').forEach(el=>{el.classList.remove('revealed');saveTaskState(el);});if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress();}function updateTestStats(prefix){const panel=document.getElementById('test-'+prefix);if(!panel)return;const radios=panel.querySelectorAll('input[type="radio"]');const groups=new Set();const correct=new Set();radios.forEach(r=>{groups.add(r.name);if(r.checked&&r.classList.contains('corr'))correct.add(r.name);});const total=groups.size;const done=correct.size;const percent=total>0?Math.round((done/total)*100):0;const statsEl=document.getElementById('stats-'+prefix);if(statsEl)statsEl.textContent='Результат: '+done+' из '+total+' ('+percent+'%)';}window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',restoreTaskState);</script></div>
<!-- 2. Лексика (практика) -->

<div id="t1-3" class="content-placeholder"><header class="lexica-header"><h1 class="lexica-title">3. Чтение</h1></header><style>.lexica-header{display:flex;justify-content:center;align-items:center;padding:0.5rem 2rem;text-align:center}.lexica-title{font-family:system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,sans-serif;font-size:clamp(2.5rem,8vw,4rem);font-weight:700;margin:0;line-height:1.1;letter-spacing:-0.02em;color:#111;animation:fadeInUp 0.8s ease-out forwards}@keyframes fadeInUp{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(20px)}to{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0)}}</style></div>
<div class="content-placeholder"><style>body{font-family:'Segoe UI',system-ui,sans-serif;background:#f8f9fa;margin:0;padding:20px;color:#334155}.wrap{max-width:900px;margin:0 0}.q-sent{font-size:16px;line-height:1.6;color:#334155;margin:0 0 12px 0;padding-left:10px;border-left:3px solid #6366f1}.opt{display:block;padding:10px 14px;margin:6px 0;background:#fff;border:2px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:8px;cursor:pointer;transition:.2s;font-size:15px;color:#475569}.opt:hover{background:#f1f5f9;border-color:#94a3b8}.opt input{margin-right:10px}.opt.corr{background:#dcfce7;border-color:#22c55e;color:#15803d;font-weight:700}.opt.wrng{background:#fee2e2;border-color:#ef4444;color:#b91c1c}.key-item{display:none;margin-top:15px;padding:12px 15px;background:#e8f5e9;border:2px solid #a5d6a7;border-radius:8px;font-size:14px;color:#2e7d32;line-height:1.6;animation:keyFade .3s}.key-item.show{display:block}.key-item b{color:#15803d;font-size:15px}.key-item small{color:#555;display:block;margin-top:5px}@keyframes keyFade{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(-6px)}to{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0)}}.btns{display:flex;gap:12px;margin-top:25px;flex-wrap:wrap}.btn{flex:1;min-width:180px;padding:16px;border:none;border-radius:10px;font-size:17px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;transition:.2s}.btn-check{background:#6366f1;color:#fff;box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(99,102,241,.3)}.btn-check:hover{background:#4f46e5;transform:translateY(-1px)}.btn-restart{background:#f59e0b;color:#fff;box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(245,158,11,.3)}.btn-restart:hover{background:#d97706;transform:translateY(-1px)}.result{display:none;margin-top:20px;padding:20px;border-radius:12px;text-align:center;font-size:18px;font-weight:700}.result.good{background:#dcfce7;color:#15803d;border:2px solid #22c55e}.result.bad{background:#fee2e2;color:#b91c1c;border:2px solid #ef4444}</style><div class="wrap" id="reading-wrap"><h1 class="title">📖 Reading: My Family</h1><div class="text"><p>I live in a small town with my family. I grew up in a big family, so our house is always noisy and fun. My parents, my two brothers and I are a nuclear family, but at holidays our extended family gets together.</p><p>My father is the eldest child in his family. He has two sisters. My grandmother on my father's side lives with us and helps my mother bring up children. My mother's parents live in another city. We visit them every summer.</p><p>I have an older brother, Tom. He is 18 and he wants to start a family soon. My younger brother, Ben, is only seven. He is the youngest child in our family. I am a middle child, so I often help my brothers make peace when they argue.</p><p>My cousins are twins — a boy and a girl. They look exactly the same! We are related to the Browns too. A distant relative of ours lives in Canada, and we sometimes write emails to him.</p><p>For our history project, we drew our family tree. It was interesting to learn about all our close relatives.</p></div><div class="q-block" data-q="1"><p class="q-label">A. Choose the TRUE sentence.</p><p class="q-sent">Which sentence is correct according to the text?</p><label class="opt"><input type="radio" name="q1" value="1" class="progress-tracker" id="q1-1" data-status="none" onchange="resetReadingTracker(this)"> 1) The writer lives in a nuclear family.</label><label class="opt"><input type="radio" name="q1" value="2" class="progress-tracker" id="q1-2" data-status="none" onchange="resetReadingTracker(this)"> 2) The writer is an only child.</label><label class="opt"><input type="radio" name="q1" value="3" class="progress-tracker" id="q1-3" data-status="none" onchange="resetReadingTracker(this)"> 3) The writer's parents live in another city.</label><div class="key-item" id="key1"><b>✅ 1) The writer lives in a nuclear family.</b><small>В тексте: «My parents, my two brothers and I are a nuclear family». Значит, писатель живёт в нуклеарной семье.</small></div></div><div class="q-block" data-q="2"><p class="q-label">B. The writer's grandmother on his father's side …</p><label class="opt"><input type="radio" name="q2" value="1" class="progress-tracker" id="q2-1" data-status="none" onchange="resetReadingTracker(this)"> 1) lives in another city.</label><label class="opt"><input type="radio" name="q2" value="2" class="progress-tracker" id="q2-2" data-status="none" onchange="resetReadingTracker(this)"> 2) lives with the family.</label><label class="opt"><input type="radio" name="q2" value="3" class="progress-tracker" id="q2-3" data-status="none" onchange="resetReadingTracker(this)"> 3) visits them every summer.</label><div class="key-item" id="key2"><b>✅ 2) lives with the family.</b><small>В тексте: «My grandmother on my father's side lives with us». Бабушка по отцовской линии живёт вместе с семьёй.</small></div></div><div class="q-block" data-q="3"><p class="q-label">C. The writer's older brother wants to …</p><label class="opt"><input type="radio" name="q3" value="1" class="progress-tracker" id="q3-1" data-status="none" onchange="resetReadingTracker(this)"> 1) draw a family tree.</label><label class="opt"><input type="radio" name="q3" value="2" class="progress-tracker" id="q3-2" data-status="none" onchange="resetReadingTracker(this)"> 2) start a family.</label><label class="opt"><input type="radio" name="q3" value="3" class="progress-tracker" id="q3-3" data-status="none" onchange="resetReadingTracker(this)"> 3) move to Canada.</label><div class="key-item" id="key3"><b>✅ 2) start a family.</b><small>В тексте: «He is 18 and he wants to start a family soon». Старший брат хочет создать семью.</small></div></div><div class="q-block" data-q="4"><p class="q-label">D. The writer's cousins are …</p><label class="opt"><input type="radio" name="q4" value="1" class="progress-tracker" id="q4-1" data-status="none" onchange="resetReadingTracker(this)"> 1) distant relatives.</label><label class="opt"><input type="radio" name="q4" value="2" class="progress-tracker" id="q4-2" data-status="none" onchange="resetReadingTracker(this)"> 2) the youngest children.</label><label class="opt"><input type="radio" name="q4" value="3" class="progress-tracker" id="q4-3" data-status="none" onchange="resetReadingTracker(this)"> 3) twins.</label><div class="key-item" id="key4"><b>✅ 3) twins.</b><small>В тексте: «My cousins are twins — a boy and a girl». Двоюродные братья/сёстры — близнецы.</small></div></div><div class="q-block" data-q="5"><p class="q-label">E. What did the family do for the history project?</p><label class="opt"><input type="radio" name="q5" value="1" class="progress-tracker" id="q5-1" data-status="none" onchange="resetReadingTracker(this)"> 1) They visited a distant relative in Canada.</label><label class="opt"><input type="radio" name="q5" value="2" class="progress-tracker" id="q5-2" data-status="none" onchange="resetReadingTracker(this)"> 2) They drew their family tree.</label><label class="opt"><input type="radio" name="q5" value="3" class="progress-tracker" id="q5-3" data-status="none" onchange="resetReadingTracker(this)"> 3) They moved to another city.</label><div class="key-item" id="key5"><b>✅ 2) They drew their family tree.</b><small>В тексте: «For our history project, we drew our family tree». Для проекта по истории они нарисовали генеалогическое древо.</small></div></div><div class="btns"><button class="btn btn-check" onclick="checkReading()">✅ Check Answers</button><button class="btn btn-restart" onclick="resetReading()">🔄 Restart</button></div><div class="result" id="reading-result"></div></div><script>if(typeof exResults!=='undefined'){exResults.reading={correct:[],wrong:[]};}var readingChecked=false;var readingAnswers={q1:'1',q2:'2',q3:'2',q4:'3',q5:'2'};function resetReadingTracker(el){if(el.classList.contains('progress-tracker')){el.dataset.status='none';}}function checkReading(){if(readingChecked)return;let score=0;const total=5;if(typeof exResults!=='undefined'){exResults.reading={correct:[],wrong:[]};}for(let q in readingAnswers){const selected=document.querySelector('input[name="'+q+'"]:checked');const qNum=q.replace('q','');const options=document.querySelectorAll('.q-block[data-q="'+qNum+'"] .opt');options.forEach(o=>o.classList.remove('corr','wrng'));if(selected){const isCorrect=selected.value===readingAnswers[q];if(isCorrect){score++;selected.parentElement.classList.add('corr');selected.dataset.status='correct';if(typeof exResults!=='undefined')exResults.reading.correct.push('q'+qNum);}else{selected.parentElement.classList.add('wrng');selected.dataset.status='none';if(typeof exResults!=='undefined')exResults.reading.wrong.push('q'+qNum);const correctOpt=document.querySelector('.q-block[data-q="'+qNum+'"] input[value="'+readingAnswers[q]+'"]');if(correctOpt)correctOpt.parentElement.classList.add('corr');}}else{const correctOpt=document.querySelector('.q-block[data-q="'+qNum+'"] input[value="'+readingAnswers[q]+'"]');if(correctOpt)correctOpt.parentElement.classList.add('corr');if(typeof exResults!=='undefined')exResults.reading.wrong.push('q'+qNum);}const key=document.getElementById('key'+qNum);if(key)key.classList.add('show');}readingChecked=true;showReadingResult(score,total);if(typeof saveProgress==='function')saveProgress();}function showReadingResult(score,total){const res=document.getElementById('reading-result');res.style.display='block';if(score===total){res.className='result good';res.innerHTML='🎉 Excellent! You got '+score+' out of '+total+' correct!';}else if(score>=3){res.className='result good';res.innerHTML='👍 Good job! You got '+score+' out of '+total+' correct.';}else{res.className='result bad';res.innerHTML='📚 Keep trying! You got '+score+' out of '+total+' correct.';}}function resetReading(){if(!confirm('Сбросить все ответы в этом задании?'))return;readingChecked=false;document.querySelectorAll('#t1-3 input[type="radio"]').forEach(r=>{r.checked=false;r.dataset.status='none';r.parentElement.classList.remove('corr','wrng');});document.querySelectorAll('#t1-3 .key-item').forEach(k=>k.classList.remove('show'));const res=document.getElementById('reading-result');res.style.display='none';res.className='result';if(typeof exResults!=='undefined'){exResults.reading={correct:[],wrong:[]};}if(typeof saveProgress==='function')saveProgress();}function restoreReadingVisuals(){if(typeof exResults!=='undefined'&&exResults.reading&&exResults.reading.correct.length>0){readingChecked=true;exResults.reading.correct.forEach(q=>{const qNum=q.replace('q','');const correctOpt=document.querySelector('.q-block[data-q="'+qNum+'"] input[value="'+readingAnswers[q]+'"]');if(correctOpt){correctOpt.checked=true;correctOpt.parentElement.classList.add('corr');}const key=document.getElementById('key'+qNum);if(key)key.classList.add('show');});exResults.reading.wrong.forEach(q=>{const qNum=q.replace('q','');const wrongOpt=document.querySelector('.q-block[data-q="'+qNum+'"] input:checked');if(wrongOpt)wrongOpt.parentElement.classList.add('wrng');const correctOpt=document.querySelector('.q-block[data-q="'+qNum+'"] input[value="'+readingAnswers[q]+'"]');if(correctOpt)correctOpt.parentElement.classList.add('corr');const key=document.getElementById('key'+qNum);if(key)key.classList.add('show');});const score=exResults.reading.correct.length;const total=5;showReadingResult(score,total);}}window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',function(){setTimeout(restoreReadingVisuals,50);});</script></div>

fbs=card.querySelectorAll('.qz-fb');for(var i=0;i<fbs.length;i++){fbs[i].classList.remove('visible');fbs[i].style.display='none';}var labels=card.querySelectorAll('.qz-opt');for(var j=0;j<labels.length;j++){labels[j].classList.remove('selected-correct','selected-wrong');}var wrap=el.parentElement;var fb=wrap.querySelector('.qz-fb');var lbl=wrap.querySelector('.qz-opt');if(fb){fb.style.display='block';fb.classList.add('visible');}if(lbl){if(el.classList.contains('corr')){lbl.classList.add('selected-correct');}else{lbl.classList.add('selected-wrong');}}if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress();}function resetTest(prefix){if(!confirm('Сбросить все ответы в этом тесте?'))return;var radios=document.querySelectorAll('input[type="radio"][name^="'+prefix+'"]');for(var i=0;i<radios.length;i++){radios[i].checked=false;}var cards=document.querySelectorAll('[id^="test-'+prefix+'"] labels=cards[i].querySelectorAll('.qz-opt');for(var j=0;j<labels.length;j++){labels[j].classList.remove('selected-correct','selected-wrong');}}var pageKey='eng_lesson_'+window.location.pathname;for(var i=0;i<radios.length;i++){localStorage.removeItem(pageKey+'_'+radios[i].id);}if(typeof updateProgress==='function')updateProgress();var
